sa pan‍nabhārau muni vippamuttauti| bhārāti tayau bhārā – khandhabhārau, kilaisabhārau, abhisaṅkhārabhārau| katamau khandhabhārau? paṭisandhiyā rūpaṃ vaidanā sañ‍ñā saṅkhārā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ khandhabhārau| katamau kilaisabhārau? rāgau dausau mauhau…pai॰… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārā – ayaṃ kilaisabhārau| katamau abhisaṅkhārabhārau? puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau – ayaṃ abhisaṅkhārabhārau| yatau khandhabhārau ca kilaisabhārau ca abhisaṅkhārabhārau ca pahīnā haunti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, sau vuc‍cati pan‍nabhārau patitabhārau auraupitabhārau samauraupitabhārau nikkhittabhārau paṭipassaddhabhārau|

munīti  maunaṃ vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ| yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā, paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūri maidhā parināyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ  amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi| taina ñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau muni maunappattau|

tīṇi maunaiyyāni – kāyamaunaiyyaṃ, vacīmaunaiyyaṃ, manaumaunaiyyaṃ| katamaṃ kāyamaunaiyyaṃ? tividhānaṃ kāyaduc‍caritānaṃ tividhakāyaduc‍caritānaṃ (ka॰) mahāni॰ 14 pahānaṃ kāyamaunaiyyaṃ, tividhaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ kāyamaunaiyyaṃ, kāyārammaṇai ñāṇaṃ kāyamaunaiyyaṃ, kāyapariñ‍ñā kāyamaunaiyyaṃ, pariñ‍ñāsahagatau maggau kāyamaunaiyyaṃ, kāyai chandarāgassa pahānaṃ kāyamaunaiyyaṃ, kāyasaṅkhāraniraudhau catutthajjhānasamāpatti kāyamaunaiyyaṃ – idaṃ kāyamaunaiyyaṃ|

katamaṃ  vacīmaunaiyyaṃ? catubbidhānaṃ vacīduc‍caritānaṃ pahānaṃ vacīmaunaiyyaṃ, catubbidhaṃ vacīsucaritaṃ vacīmaunaiyyaṃ, vācārammaṇai ñāṇaṃ vacīmaunaiyyaṃ, vācāpariñ‍ñā vacīmaunaiyyaṃ, pariñ‍ñāsahagatau maggau vacīmaunaiyyaṃ, vācāya chandarāgassa pahānaṃ vacīmaunaiyyaṃ, vacīsaṅkhāraniraudhau dutiyajjhānasamāpatti vacīmaunaiyyaṃ – idaṃ vacīmaunaiyyaṃ|

katamaṃ  manaumaunaiyyaṃ? tividhānaṃ manauduc‍caritānaṃ pahānaṃ manaumaunaiyyaṃ, tividhaṃ manausucaritaṃ manaumaunaiyyaṃ, cittārammaṇai  ñāṇaṃ manaumaunaiyyaṃ, cittapariñ‍ñā manaumaunaiyyaṃ, pariñ‍ñāsahagatau maggau manaumaunaiyyaṃ, cittai chandarāgassa pahānaṃ manaumaunaiyyaṃ, cittasaṅkhāraniraudhau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpatti manaumaunaiyyaṃ – idaṃ manaumaunaiyyaṃ|

‘‘kāyamuniṃ vācāmuniṃ, manaumunimanāsavaṃ|

muniṃ maunaiyyasampan‍naṃ, āhu sabbappahāyinaṃ||

‘‘kāyamuniṃ vācāmuniṃ, manaumunimanāsavaṃ|

muniṃ maunaiyyasampan‍naṃ, āhu ninhātapāpaka’’nti ninahātapāpakanti (sī॰)||

imaihi  tīhi maunaiyyaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā cha muninau munayau (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) – agāramuninau, anagāramuninau, saikhamuninau, asaikhamuninau, pac‍caikamuninau, munimuninauti munimuninau (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) mahāni॰ 14| katamai agāramuninau? yai tai agārikā diṭṭhapadā viñ‍ñātasāsanā – imai agāramuninau| katamai anagāramuninau? yai tai pabbajitā diṭṭhapadā viñ‍ñātasāsanā – imai anagāramuninau| satta saikhā saikhamuninau, arahantau asaikhamuninau| pac‍caikabuddhā pac‍caikamuninau| munimuninau vuc‍canti tathāgatā arahantau sammāsambuddhā|

‘‘na maunaina muni hauti, mūḷharūpau aviddasu|

yau ca tulaṃva paggayha, varamādāya paṇḍitau||

‘‘pāpāni  parivaj‍jaiti, sa muni taina sau muni|

yau munāti ubhau laukai, muni taina pavuc‍cati||

‘‘asatañ‍ca  satañ‍ca ñatvā dhammaṃ, ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca sabbalaukai|

daivamanussaihi pūjitau yau, saṅgajālamatic‍ca sau muni’’||

vippamuttauti muninau rāgā cittaṃ muttaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttaṃ; dausā cittaṃ… mauhā cittaṃ muttaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttaṃ…pai॰… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhāraihi cittaṃ muttaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti – sa pan‍nabhārau muni vippamuttau|

na kappiyau nūparatau na patthiyauti bhagavāti| kappāti dvai kappā – taṇhākappau ca diṭṭhikappau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhākappau…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhikappau| tassa taṇhākappau pahīnau, diṭṭhikappau paṭinissaṭṭhau; taṇhākappassa pahīnattā  diṭṭhikappassa paṭinissaṭṭhattā taṇhākappaṃ  vā diṭṭhikappaṃ vā na kappaiti na janaiti na sañ‍janaiti na nibbattaiti nābhinibbattaitīti – na kappiyau| nūparatauti| sabbai bālaputhuj‍janā raj‍janti, puthuj‍janakalyāṇakaṃ upādāya satta saikhā appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya āramanti viramanti paṭiviramanti, arahā āratau viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau  vimariyādikataina caitasā viharatīti – na kappiyau nūparatau| na patthiyauti| patthanā vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| yassaisā patthanā taṇhā pahīnā samucchin‍nā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, sau vuc‍cati na patthiyau|

bhagavāti gāravādhivacanaṃ| api ca bhaggarāgauti bhagavā, bhaggadausauti bhagavā, bhaggamauhauti bhagavā, bhaggamānauti bhagavā, bhaggadiṭṭhīti bhagavā, bhaggakaṇḍakauti bhagavā, bhaggakilaisauti bhagavā, bhaji vibhaji pavibhaji dhammaratananti bhagavā, bhavānaṃ antakarauti bhagavā, bhāvitakāyau bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñauti bhagavā; bhāgī vā bhagavā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppānīti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā attharasassa dhammarasassa vimuttirasassa adhisīlassa adhicittassa adhipañ‍ñāyāti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ appamañ‍ñānaṃ catun‍naṃ arūpasamāpattīnanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā aṭṭhan‍naṃ vimaukkhānaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ abhibhāyatanānaṃ navan‍naṃ anupubbavihārasamāpattīnanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā  dasan‍naṃ sañ‍ñābhāvanānaṃ dasan‍naṃ kasiṇasamāpattīnaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhissa asubhasamāpattiyāti  bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā  catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassāti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā dasan‍naṃ tathāgatabalānaṃ catun‍naṃ vaisāraj‍jānaṃ catun‍naṃ paṭisambhidānaṃ chan‍naṃ abhiñ‍ñānaṃ chan‍naṃ buddhadhammānanti bhagavā, bhagavāti naitaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ na pitarā kataṃ na bhātarā kataṃ na bhaginiyā kataṃ na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ na ñātisālauhitaihi  kataṃ na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ na daivatāhi kataṃ; vimaukkhantikamaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ baudhiyā mūlai saha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassa paṭilābhā sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ bhagavāti – na kappiyau nūparatau na patthiyau iti bhagavā|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘sa sabbadhammaisu visainibhūtau, yaṃ kiñ‍ci diṭṭhaṃ va sutaṃ mutaṃ vā|

sa pan‍nabhārau muni vippamuttau, na kappiyau nūparatau na patthiyau’’||[iti bhagavāti]

mahāviyūhasuttaniddaisau tairasamau|



14. tuvaṭṭakasuttaniddaisau

atha  tuvaṭṭakasuttaniddaisaṃ vakkhati –

150.

 pucchāmi  taṃ ādic‍cabandhu, ādic‍cabandhū (sī॰ syā॰) vivaikaṃ santipadañ‍ca mahaisi mahaisiṃ (sī॰ syā॰) |

kathaṃ disvā nibbāti bhikkhu, anupādiyānau laukasmiṃ kiñ‍ci||

pucchāmi taṃ ādic‍cabandhūti| pucchāti tissau pucchā – adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā, diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā, vimaticchaidanā pucchā| katamā adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā? pakatiyā lakkhaṇaṃ añ‍ñātaṃ hauti adiṭṭhaṃ atulitaṃ atīritaṃ avibhūtaṃ avibhāvitaṃ, tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya tulanāya tīraṇāya vibhāvanāya pañhaṃ pucchati – ayaṃ adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā| katamā diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā? pakatiyā lakkhaṇaṃ ñātaṃ hauti diṭṭhaṃ tulitaṃ tīritaṃ vibhūtaṃ vibhāvitaṃ, añ‍ñaihi paṇḍitaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati – ayaṃ diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā| katamā vimaticchaidanā pucchā? pakatiyā saṃsayapakkhandau saṃsayapakkhan‍nau (sī॰ syā॰) hauti vimatipakkhandau dvaiḷhakajātau, ‘‘aivaṃ nu khau, na nu khau, kiṃ nu khau, kathaṃ nu khau’’ti sau vimaticchaidanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati – ayaṃ vimaticchaidanā pucchā| imā tissau pucchā|

aparāpi  tissau pucchā – manussapucchā, amanussapucchā, nimmitapucchā| katamā  manussapucchā? manussā buddhaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, bhikkhū pucchanti, bhikkhuniyau pucchanti, upāsakā pucchanti, upāsikāyau pucchanti, rājānau pucchanti, khattiyā pucchanti, brāhmaṇā pucchanti, vaissā pucchanti, suddā pucchanti, gahaṭṭhā pucchanti, pabbajitā pucchanti – ayaṃ manussapucchā| katamā amanussapucchā? amanussā buddhaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, nāgā pucchanti, supaṇṇā pucchanti, yakkhā pucchanti, asurā pucchanti, gandhabbā pucchanti, mahārājānau pucchanti, indā pucchanti, brahmānau pucchanti, daivatāyau pucchanti – ayaṃ amanussapucchā| katamā nimmitapucchā? yaṃ bhagavā rūpaṃ abhinimmināti manaumayaṃ sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgaṃ ahīnindriyaṃ taṃ sau nimmitau buddhaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā  pañhaṃ pucchati, bhagavā tassa natthi sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu visaj‍jaiti – ayaṃ nimmitapucchā| imā tissau pucchā|

aparāpi  tissau pucchā – attatthapucchā, paratthapucchā, ubhayatthapucchā| aparāpi tissau pucchā – diṭṭhadhammikatthapucchā, samparāyikatthapucchā, paramatthapucchā| aparāpi tissau pucchā – anavaj‍jatthapucchā, nik‍kilaisatthapucchā nikkhaipatthapucchā (sī॰ ka॰), vaudānatthapucchā| aparāpi tissau pucchā – atītapucchā, anāgatapucchā, pac‍cuppan‍napucchā| aparāpi tissau pucchā – ajjhattapucchā, bahiddhāpucchā, ajjhattabahiddhāpucchā| aparāpi tissau pucchā – kusalapucchā, akusalapucchā, abyākatapucchā| aparāpi tissau pucchā – khandhapucchā, dhātupucchā, āyatanapucchā| aparāpi tissau pucchā – satipaṭṭhānapucchā, sammappadhānapucchā, iddhipādapucchā| aparāpi  tissau pucchā – indriyapucchā, balapucchā, baujjhaṅgapucchā| aparāpi tissau pucchā – maggapucchā, phalapucchā, nibbānapucchā|

pucchāmi tanti pucchāmi taṃ yācāmi taṃ ajjhaisāmi taṃ pasādaimi taṃ, ‘‘kathayassu mai’’ti – pucchāmi taṃ| ādic‍cabandhūti| ādic‍cau vuc‍cati sūriyau suriyau (sī॰ syā॰)| sūriyau gautamau gauttaina, bhagavāpi gautamau gauttaina, bhagavā sūriyassa gauttañātakau gauttabandhu; tasmā buddhau ādic‍cabandhūti – pucchāmi taṃ ādic‍cabandhu|

vivaikaṃ santipadañ‍ca mahaisīti| vivaikāti tayau vivaikā – kāyavivaikau, cittavivaikau, upadhivivaikau| katamau kāyavivaikau? idha  bhikkhu vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ kāyaina vivittaina viharati| sau aikau gacchati, aikau tiṭṭhati, aikau nisīdati, aikau saiyyaṃ kappaiti, aikau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisati, aikau paṭik‍kamati, aikau rahau nisīdati, aikau caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, aikau carati viharati iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti – ayaṃ kāyavivaikau|

katamau cittavivaikau? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa nīvaraṇaihi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicāraihi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa pītiyā cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa sukhadukkhaihi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa rūpasañ‍ñāya paṭighasañ‍ñāya nānattasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  samāpan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāya cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti| sautāpan‍nassa sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā vicikicchāya sīlabbataparāmāsā diṭṭhānusayā vicikicchānusayā, tadaikaṭṭhaihi ca kilaisaihi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti| sakadāgāmissa auḷārikā kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanā  paṭighasañ‍ñaujanā auḷārikā kāmarāgānusayā paṭighānusayā, tadaikaṭṭhaihi ca kilaisaihi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti| anāgāmissa anusahagatā kāmarāgasañ‍ñaujanā paṭighasañ‍ñaujanā anusahagatā kāmarāgānusayā paṭighānusayā, tadaikaṭṭhaihi ca kilaisaihi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti| arahatau rūparāgā arūparāgā mānā uddhac‍cā avij‍jāya mānānusayā bhavarāgānusayā avij‍jānusayā, tadaikaṭṭhaihi ca kilaisaihi bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaihi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hauti – ayaṃ cittavivaikau|

katamau upadhivivaikau? upadhi vuc‍cati kilaisā ca khandhā ca abhisaṅkhārā ca| upadhivivaikau vuc‍cati amataṃ nibbānaṃ| yau sau sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ – ayaṃ upadhivivaikau| kāyavivaikau ca vivaikaṭṭhakāyānaṃ vūpakaṭṭhakāyānaṃ (sī॰) naikkhammābhiratānaṃ, cittavivaikau ca parisuddhacittānaṃ paramavaudānapattānaṃ, upadhivivaikau ca  nirupadhīnaṃ puggalānaṃ visaṅkhāragatānaṃ| santīti aikaina ākāraina santipi santipadampi taṃyaiva amataṃ nibbānaṃ, yau sau sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘santamaitaṃ padaṃ, paṇītamaitaṃ padaṃ, yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’’nti| atha aparaina  ākāraina yai dhammā santādhigamāya  santiphusanāya santisacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti, saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau – imai vuc‍canti santipadaṃ tāṇapadaṃ laiṇapadaṃ saraṇapadaṃ abhayapadaṃ ac‍cutapadaṃ amatapadaṃ nibbānapadaṃ|

mahaisīti mahaisi bhagavā| mahantaṃ sīlakkhandhaṃ aisī gavaisī pariyaisīti mahaisi, mahantaṃ samādhikkhandhaṃ…pai॰… mahantaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ… mahantaṃ vimuttikkhandhaṃ… mahantaṃ vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ aisī gavaisī pariyaisīti mahaisi; mahatau tamaukāyassa padālanaṃ, mahatau vipal‍lāsassa bhaidanaṃ, mahatau taṇhāsal‍lassa abbahanaṃ abbūhanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), abbhuhanaṃ (ka॰), mahatau diṭṭhisaṅghātassa vinivaiṭhanaṃ, mahatau mānadhajassa papātanaṃ pavāhanaṃ (syā॰), mahatau abhisaṅkhārassa vūpasamaṃ, mahatau aughassa nittharaṇaṃ, mahatau bhārassa nikkhaipanaṃ, mahatau saṃsāravaṭṭassa upacchaidaṃ, mahatau santāpassa nibbāpanaṃ, mahatau pariḷāhassa paṭipassaddhiṃ, mahatau dhammadhajassa ussāpanaṃ aisī gavaisī pariyaisīti mahaisi, mahantai satipaṭṭhānai mahantai sammappadhānai mahantai iddhipādai mahantāni indriyāni mahantāni balāni mahantai baujjhaṅgai mahantaṃ ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ mahantaṃ paramatthaṃ amataṃ nibbānaṃ aisī gavaisī pariyaisīti mahaisi; mahaisakkhaihi vā sattaihi aisitau gavaisitau pariyaisitau kahaṃ  buddhau kahaṃ bhagavā kahaṃ daivadaivau kahaṃ narāsabhauti mahaisīti – vivaikaṃ santipadañ‍ca mahaisi|

kathaṃ disvā nibbāti bhikkhūti| kathaṃ disvā passitvā tulayitvā  tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā attanau rāgaṃ nibbāpaiti, dausaṃ nibbāpaiti, mauhaṃ nibbāpaiti, kaudhaṃ…pai॰… upanāhaṃ… makkhaṃ… paḷāsaṃ… issaṃ… macchariyaṃ… māyaṃ… sāṭhaiyyaṃ… thambhaṃ… sārambhaṃ… mānaṃ… atimānaṃ… madaṃ… pamādaṃ… sabbai kilaisai… sabbai duc‍caritai… sabbai darathai… sabbai pariḷāhai… sabbai santāpai … sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārai nibbāpaiti samaiti upasamaiti vūpasamaiti paṭipassambhaiti| bhikkhūti puthuj‍janakalyāṇakau vā bhikkhu saikhau vā bhikkhūti – kathaṃ disvā nibbāti bhikkhu|

anupādiyānau laukasmiṃ kiñ‍cīti| catūhi upādānaihi anupādiyamānau agaṇhayamānau aparāmasamānau anabhinivisamānau| laukasminti apāyalaukai manussalaukai daivalaukai, khandhalaukai dhātulaukai āyatanalaukai| kiñ‍cīti kiñ‍ci rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagatanti – anupādiyānau laukasmiṃ kiñ‍ci|

taināha sau nimmitau –

‘‘pucchāmi taṃ ādic‍cabandhu, vivaikaṃ santipadañ‍ca mahaisi|

kathaṃ disvā nibbāti bhikkhu, anupādiyānau laukasmiṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti||

151.

 mūlaṃ  papañ‍casaṅkhāya, [iti bhagavā]

mantā asmīti sabbamuparundhai sabbamuparuddhai (syā॰) |

yā kāci taṇhā ajjhattaṃ, tāsaṃ vinayā sadā satau sikkhai||

mūlaṃ papañ‍casaṅkhāya, [iti bhagavā] mantā asmīti sabbamuparundhaiti| papañ‍cāyaiva papañ‍casaṅkhā| taṇhāpapañ‍casaṅkhā diṭṭhipapañ‍casaṅkhā| katamaṃ taṇhāpapañ‍cassa mūlaṃ? avij‍jāmūlaṃ, ayaunisaumanasikārau  mūlaṃ, asmimānau mūlaṃ, ahirikaṃ mūlaṃ, anauttappaṃ mūlaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ mūlaṃ – idaṃ taṇhāpapañ‍cassa mūlaṃ| katamaṃ diṭṭhipapañ‍cassa mūlaṃ? avij‍jāmūlaṃ, ayaunisaumanasikārau mūlaṃ, asmimānau mūlaṃ, ahirikaṃ mūlaṃ, anauttappaṃ mūlaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ mūlaṃ – idaṃ diṭṭhipapañ‍cassa mūlaṃ|

bhagavāti gāravādhivacanaṃ| api ca bhaggarāgauti bhagavā, bhaggadausauti bhagavā, bhaggamauhauti bhagavā, bhaggamānauti bhagavā, bhaggadiṭṭhīti bhagavā, bhaggakaṇḍakauti bhagavā, bhaggakilaisauti bhagavā, bhaji vibhaji pavibhaji dhammaratananti bhagavā, bhavānaṃ antakarauti bhagavā, bhāvitakāyau bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñauti bhagavā; bhāgī vā bhagavā  arañ‍ñavanapatthāni  pantāni saināsanāni appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppānīti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā attharasassa dhammarasassa vimuttirasassa adhisīlassa adhicittassa adhipañ‍ñāyāti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ appamañ‍ñānaṃ catun‍naṃ arūpasamāpattīnanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā aṭṭhan‍naṃ vimaukkhānaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ abhibhāyatanānaṃ navan‍naṃ anupubbavihārasamāpattīnanti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā dasan‍naṃ sañ‍ñābhāvanānaṃ dasan‍naṃ kasiṇasamāpattīnaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhissa asubhasamāpattiyāti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassāti bhagavā, bhāgī vā bhagavā dasan‍naṃ tathāgatabalānaṃ catun‍naṃ vaisāraj‍jānaṃ catun‍naṃ paṭisambhidānaṃ chan‍naṃ abhiñ‍ñānaṃ chan‍naṃ buddhadhammānanti  bhagavā, bhagavāti naitaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ na pitarā kataṃ na bhātarā kataṃ na bhaginiyā kataṃ na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ na daivatāhi kataṃ; vimaukkhantikamaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ baudhiyā mūlai saha sabbañ‍ñutañāṇassa paṭilābhā sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ bhagavāti – mūlaṃ papañ‍casaṅkhāya iti bhagavā|

mantā asmīti sabbamuparundhaiti| mantā vuc‍cati pañ‍ñā| yā pañ‍ñā  pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi| asmīti rūpai asmīti mānau asmīti chandau asmīti anusayau; vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai asmīti mānau asmīti chandau asmīti anusayauti| mūlaṃ papañ‍casaṅkhāya iti bhagavā| mantā asmīti sabbamuparundhaiti| papañ‍casaṅkhāya mūlañ‍ca asmimānañ‍ca mantāya sabbaṃ rundhaiyya uparundhaiyya niraudhaiyya vūpasamaiyya atthaṅgamaiyya  paṭipassambhaiyyāti – mūlaṃ papañ‍casaṅkhāya iti bhagavā, mantā asmīti sabbamuparundhai|

yā kāci taṇhā ajjhattanti| yā kācīti sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asaisaṃ nissaisaṃ pariyādiyanavacanamaitaṃ – yā kācīti| taṇhāti rūpataṇhā…pai॰… dhammataṇhā| ajjhattanti ajjhattasamuṭṭhānā vā ajjhattaṃ samuṭṭhāti (syā॰) sā taṇhāti – ajjhattaṃ| atha vā ajjhattikaṃ vuc‍cati cittaṃ| yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| cittaina sā taṇhā sahagatā sahajātā saṃsaṭṭhā sampayuttā aikuppādā aikaniraudhā aikavatthukā aikārammaṇātipi ajjhattanti – yā kāci taṇhā ajjhattaṃ|

tāsaṃ vinayā sadā satau sikkhaiti| sadāti sadā sabbadā  sabbakālaṃ nic‍cakālaṃ dhuvakālaṃ, satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ pauṅkhānupauṅkhaṃ udakūmigajātaṃ avīcisantatisahitaṃ phusitaṃ, puraibhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ, purimaṃ yāmaṃ majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ, kāḷai juṇhai, vassai haimantai gimhai, purimai vayaukhandhai majjhimai vayaukhandhai pacchimai vayaukhandhai| satauti catūhi kāraṇaihi satau – kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvaintau satau, vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvaintau satau| aparaihipi catūhi kāraṇaihi satau – asati parivaj‍janāya satau, satikaraṇīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ katattā satau, satipaṭipakkhānaṃ dhammānaṃ hatattā satau, satinimittānaṃ asammuṭṭhattā satau| aparaihipi catūhi kāraṇaihi satau – satiyā saman‍nāgatattā satau, satiyā vasitattā satau, satiyā pāguñ‍ñatāya satau, satiyā apac‍caurauhanatāya satau| aparaihipi catūhi kāraṇaihi satau – sattattā satau, santattā satau, samitattā satau, santadhammasaman‍nāgatattā satau| buddhānussatiyā satau, dhammānussatiyā satau, saṅghānussatiyā satau, sīlānussatiyā satau, cāgānussatiyā satau, daivatānussatiyā satau, ānāpānassatiyā satau, maraṇassatiyā satau, kāyagatāsatiyā satau, upasamānussatiyā satau| yā sati anussati paṭissati, sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā, sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau aikāyanamaggau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sati| imāya satiyā upaitau samupaitau, upagatau samupagatau, upapan‍nau samupapan‍nau, saman‍nāgatau sau vuc‍cati satau|

sikkhaiti  tissau sikkhā – adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| katamā  adhisīlasikkhā? idha bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu  bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, khuddakau sīlakkhandhau… mahantau  sīlakkhandhau, sīlaṃ patiṭṭhā ādi caraṇaṃ saṃyamau saṃvarau mukhaṃ pamukhaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – ayaṃ adhisīlasikkhā|

katamā adhicittasikkhā? idha bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ adhicittasikkhā|

katamā adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā? idha bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā| sau idaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, imai āsavāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavasamudayauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavaniraudhauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā|

tāsaṃ vinayā sadā satau sikkhaiti| tāsaṃ taṇhānaṃ vinayāya paṭivinayāya pahānāya vūpasamāya paṭinissaggāya paṭipassaddhiyā adhisīlampi sikkhaiyya, adhicittampi sikkhaiyya, adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhaiyya; imā tissau sikkhāyau āvaj‍jantau sikkhaiyya, pajānantau sikkhaiyya, passantau  sikkhaiyya, pac‍cavaikkhantau sikkhaiyya, cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahantau sikkhaiyya, saddhāya adhimuc‍cantau sikkhaiyya, vīriyaṃ paggaṇhantau sikkhaiyya, satiṃ upaṭṭhapaintau sikkhaiyya, cittaṃ samādahantau sikkhaiyya, pañ‍ñāya pajānantau sikkhaiyya, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau sikkhaiyya, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau sikkhaiyya, pahātabbaṃ pajahantau sikkhaiyya, bhāvaitabbaṃ bhāvaintau sikkhaiyya, sacchikātabbaṃ  sacchikarauntau sikkhaiyya ācaraiyya samācaraiyya samādāya vattaiyyāti – tāsaṃ vinayā sadā satau sikkhai|

taināha  bhagavā –

‘‘mūlaṃ papañ‍casaṅkhāya, [iti bhagavā]

mantā asmīti sabbamuparundhai|

yā kāci taṇhā ajjhattaṃ, tāsaṃ vinayā sadā satau sikkhai’’ti||

152.

 yaṃ  kiñ‍ci dhammamabhijañ‍ñā, ajjhattaṃ atha vāpi bahiddhā|

na taina thāmaṃ kubbaitha, na hi sā nibbuti sataṃ vuttā||

yaṃ kiñ‍ci dhammamabhijañ‍ñā ajjhattanti| yaṃ kiñ‍ci attanau guṇaṃ jānaiyya kusalai vā dhammai abyākatai vā dhammai| katamai attanau guṇā? uc‍cā kulā pabbajitau vā assaṃ assa (syā॰), mahābhaugakulā pabbajitau vā assaṃ, uḷārabhaugakulā pabbajitau vā assaṃ, ñātau yasassī sagahaṭṭhapabbajitānanti vā assaṃ, lābhimhi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānanti vā assaṃ, suttantikau vā assaṃ, vinayadharau vā assaṃ, dhammakathikau vā assaṃ, ārañ‍ñikau vā assaṃ, piṇḍapātikau vā assaṃ, paṃsukūlikau vā assaṃ, taicīvarikau vā assaṃ, sapadānacārikau vā assaṃ, khalupacchābhattikau vā assaṃ, naisaj‍jikau vā assaṃ, yathāsanthatikau vā assaṃ, paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhīti vā assaṃ, dutiyassa jhānassa lābhīti vā assaṃ, tatiyassa jhānassa  lābhīti vā assaṃ, catutthassa jhānassa lābhīti vā assaṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhīti vā assaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhīti vā assaṃ – imai vuc‍canti attanau guṇā| yaṃ kiñ‍ci attanau guṇaṃ jānaiyya ājānaiyya vijānaiyya paṭivijānaiyya paṭivijjhaiyyāti – yaṃ kiñ‍ci dhammamabhijañ‍ñā ajjhattaṃ| atha vāpi bahiddhāti| upajjhāyassa vā ācariyassa vā tai guṇā assūti assūti ajjhattaṃ (bahūsu) – atha vāpi bahiddhā|

na  taina thāmaṃ kubbaithāti| attanau vā guṇaina paraisaṃ vā guṇaina thāmaṃ na karaiyya, thambhaṃ na karaiyya, mānaṃ na karaiyya, un‍natiṃ na karaiyya, un‍namaṃ na karaiyya, na taina mānaṃ janaiyya, na taina thaddhau assa patthaddhau paggahitasirauti – na taina thāmaṃ kubbaitha|

na hi sā nibbuti sataṃ vuttāti| satānaṃ santānaṃ sappurisānaṃ buddhānaṃ buddhasāvakānaṃ pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ sā nibbutīti na vuttā na pavuttā na ācikkhitā na daisitā na pañ‍ñapitā  na paṭṭhapitā na vivaṭā na vibhattā na uttānīkatā nappakāsitāti – na hi sā nibbuti sataṃ vuttā|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dhammamabhijañ‍ñā, ajjhattaṃ atha vāpi bahiddhā|

na taina thāmaṃ kubbaitha, na hi sā nibbuti sataṃ vuttā’’ti||

153.

 saiyyau na taina mañ‍ñaiyya, nīcaiyyau atha vāpi sarikkhau|

phuṭṭhau  anaikarūpaihi, nātumānaṃ vikappayaṃ tiṭṭhai||

saiyyau na taina mañ‍ñaiyyāti| ‘‘saiyyauhamasmī’’ti atimānaṃ na janaiyya jātiyā vā gauttaina vā kaulaputtiyaina vā vaṇṇapaukkharatāya  vā dhanaina vā ajjhainaina vā kammāyatanaina vā sippāyatanaina vā vij‍jāṭṭhānaina vā sutaina vā paṭibhānaina vā añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunāti – saiyyau na taina mañ‍ñaiyya|

nīcaiyyau atha vāpi sarikkhauti| ‘‘hīnauhamasmī’’ti aumānaṃ na janaiyya jātiyā vā gauttaina vā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunā| ‘‘sadisauhamasmī’’ti mānaṃ na janaiyya jātiyā vā gauttaina vā kaulaputtiyaina vā vaṇṇapaukkharatāya vā dhanaina vā ajjhainaina vā kammāyatanaina vā sippāyatanaina vā vij‍jāṭṭhānaina vā sutaina vā paṭibhānaina vā añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunāti – nīcaiyyau atha vāpi sarikkhau|

phuṭṭhau anaikarūpaihīti| anaikavidhaihi ākāraihi phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatauti – phuṭṭhau anaikarūpaihi|

nātumānaṃ  vikappayaṃ tiṭṭhaiti| ātumā vuc‍cati attā| attānaṃ kappaintau vikappaintau vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantau na tiṭṭhaiyyāti – nātumānaṃ vikappayaṃ tiṭṭhai|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘saiyyau  na taina mañ‍ñaiyya, nīcaiyyau atha vāpi sarikkhau|

phuṭṭhau anaikarūpaihi, nātumānaṃ vikappayaṃ tiṭṭhai’’ti||

154.

 ajjhattamaivupasamai, na añ‍ñatau bhikkhu santimaisaiyya| 

ajjhattaṃ upasantassa, natthi attā attaṃ (syā॰) kutau nirattā nirattaṃ (syā॰) vā||

ajjhattamaivupasamaiti| ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ samaiyya, dausaṃ samaiyya, mauhaṃ samaiyya, kaudhaṃ…pai॰… upanāhaṃ… makkhaṃ… paḷāsaṃ… issaṃ… macchariyaṃ… māyaṃ… sāṭhaiyyaṃ… thambhaṃ… sārambhaṃ  … mānaṃ… atimānaṃ… madaṃ… pamādaṃ… sabbai kilaisai… sabbai duc‍caritai… sabbai darathai… sabbai pariḷāhai… sabbai santāpai… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārai samaiyya upasamaiyya vūpasamaiyya nibbāpaiyya paṭipassambhaiyyāti – ajjhattamaivupasamai|

na añ‍ñatau bhikkhu santimaisaiyyāti| añ‍ñatau asuddhimaggaina, micchāpaṭipadāya, aniyyānapataina, añ‍ñatra satipaṭṭhānaihi, añ‍ñatra sammappadhānaihi, añ‍ñatra iddhipādaihi, añ‍ñatra indriyaihi, añ‍ñatra balaihi, añ‍ñatra baujjhaṅgaihi, añ‍ñatra ariyā aṭṭhaṅgikā maggā santiṃ upasantiṃ vūpasantiṃ nibbutiṃ paṭipassaddhiṃ na aisaiyya na gavaisaiyya na pariyaisaiyyāti – na añ‍ñatau bhikkhu santimaisaiyya|

ajjhattaṃ  upasantassāti| ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ santassa, dausaṃ santassa, mauhaṃ santassa…pai॰… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārai santassa upasantassa vūpasantassa nibbutassa paṭipassaddhiyāti – ajjhattaṃ upasantassa|

natthi attā kutau nirattā vāti| natthīti paṭikkhaipau| attāti attadiṭṭhi natthi; nirattāti ucchaidadiṭṭhi natthi| attāti gahitaṃ natthi; nirattāti muñ‍citabbaṃ natthi| yassatthi gahitaṃ, tassatthi muñ‍citabbaṃ| tassa gahitaṃ gāhaṃ  muñ‍canaṃ samatik‍kantau arahā vuddhipārihānivītivattau| sau vuṭṭhavāsau ciṇṇacaraṇau…pai॰… jātimaraṇasaṃsārau, natthi tassa punabbhavauti – natthi attā kutau nirattā vā|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘ajjhattamaivupasamai, na añ‍ñatau bhikkhu santimaisaiyya|

ajjhattaṃ upasantassa, natthi attā kutau nirattā vā’’ti||

155.

 majjhai  yathā samuddassa, ūmi nau jāyatī ṭhitau hauti|

aivaṃ ṭhitau anaijassa, ussadaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya karai (sī॰) kuhiñ‍ci||

majjhai yathā samuddassa, ūmi nau jāyatī ṭhitau hautīti| samuddau caturāsītiyaujanasahassāni ubbaidhaina gambhīrau| haiṭṭhā cattārīsayaujanasahassāni  udakaṃ macchakacchapaihi kampati| upari cattārīsayaujanasahassāni udakaṃ vātaihi kampati| majjhai cattārīsayaujanasahassāni udakaṃ na kampati na vikampati na calati na vaidhati nappavaidhati na sampavaidhati| anairitau aghaṭṭitau acalitau aluḷitau abhantau vūpasantau tatra ūmi nau jāyati, ṭhitau hauti samuddauti| aivampi majjhai yathā samuddassa ūmi nau jāyatī ṭhitau hauti|

atha vā sattan‍naṃ pabbatānaṃ antarikāsu sattasīdantarā mahāsamuddā| sīdantarasamuddau (syā॰) tatra udakaṃ na kampati na vikampati na calati na vaidhati nappavaidhati na sampavaidhati| anairitau aghaṭṭitau acalitau aluḷitau abhantau vūpasantau tatra ūmi nau jāyati, ṭhitau hauti samuddauti| aivampi majjhai yathā samuddassa ūmi nau jāyatī ṭhitau hauti|

aivaṃ ṭhitau anaijassāti| aivanti aupammasampaṭipādanaṃ| ṭhitauti lābhaipi na kampati, alābhaipi na kampati, yasaipi na kampati, ayasaipi na kampati, pasaṃsāyapi na kampati, nindāyapi na kampati, sukhaipi na kampati, dukkhaipi na kampati na vikampati na calati na vaidhati nappavaidhati na sampavaidhatīti – aivaṃ ṭhitau| anaijassāti aijā vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau  sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| yassaisā aijā taṇhā pahīnā ucchin‍nā samucchin‍nā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, sau vuc‍cati anaijau| aijāya pahīnattā anaijau; sau lābhaipi na iñ‍jati, alābhaipi na iñ‍jati, yasaipi na iñ‍jati, ayasaipi  na iñ‍jati, pasaṃsāyapi na iñ‍jati, nindāyapi na iñ‍jati, sukhaipi na iñ‍jati, dukkhaipi na iñ‍jati, na calati na vaidhati nappavaidhati na sampavaidhatīti – aivaṃ ṭhitau anaijassa|

ussadaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍cīti| ussadāti sattussadā – rāgussadaṃ rāgussadau (syā॰), dausussadaṃ, mauhussadaṃ, mānussadaṃ, diṭṭhussadaṃ, kilaisussadaṃ, kammussadaṃ na karaiyya na janaiyya na sañ‍janaiyya na  nibbattaiyya nābhinibbattaiyya| kuhiñ‍cīti kuhiñ‍ci kismiñ‍ci katthaci ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā ajjhattabahiddhā vāti – ussadaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘majjhai yathā samuddassa, ūmi nau jāyatī ṭhitau hauti|

aivaṃ ṭhitau anaijassa, ussadaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍cī’’ti||

156.

 akittayī vivaṭacakkhu, sakkhidhammaṃ parissayavinayaṃ| 

paṭipadaṃ vadaihi bhaddantai, pātimaukkhaṃ atha vāpi samādhiṃ||

akittayī vivaṭacakkhūti| akittayīti kittitaṃ ācikkhitaṃ daisitaṃ pañ‍ñapitaṃ paṭṭhapitaṃ vivaṭaṃ vibhattaṃ uttānīkataṃ pakāsitanti  – akittayi akittayīti akittayi parikittayi ācikkhi daisaisi pañ‍ñapaisi paṭṭhapaisi vivari vibhaji uttānimakāsi pakāsaisīti akittayi (syā॰)| vivaṭacakkhūti bhagavā pañ‍cahi cakkhūhi vivaṭacakkhu – maṃsacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu, dibbaina cakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu, pañ‍ñācakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu, buddhacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu, samantacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu|

kathaṃ bhagavā maṃsacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu? maṃsacakkhumhipi bhagavatau pañ‍ca vaṇṇā saṃvij‍janti – nīlau ca vaṇṇau, pītakau ca vaṇṇau, lauhitakau ca  vaṇṇau, kaṇhau ca vaṇṇau, audātau  ca vaṇṇau| akkhilaumāni ca bhagavatau yattha ca akkhilaumāni patiṭṭhitāni taṃ nīlaṃ hauti sunīlaṃ pāsādikaṃ dassanaiyyaṃ umāpupphasamānaṃ ummāpupphasamānaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), ummārapupphasamānaṃ (syā॰)| tassa paratau pītakaṃ hauti supītakaṃ suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ pāsādikaṃ dassanaiyyaṃ kaṇikārapupphasamānaṃ| ubhayatau ca akkhikūṭāni bhagavatau lauhitakāni haunti sulauhitakāni pāsādikāni dassanaiyyāni indagaupakasamānāni| majjhai kaṇhaṃ hauti sukaṇhaṃ alūkhaṃ siniddhaṃ pāsādikaṃ dassanaiyyaṃ addāriṭṭhakasamānaṃ aḷāriṭṭhakasamānaṃ (syā॰)| tassa paratau audātaṃ hauti suaudātaṃ saitaṃ paṇḍaraṃ pāsādikaṃ dassanaiyyaṃ ausadhitārakasamānaṃ| taina bhagavā pākatikaina maṃsacakkhunā attabhāvapariyāpan‍naina purimasucaritakammābhinibbattaina samantā yaujanaṃ passati divā caiva rattiñ‍ca| yadā hipi caturaṅgasaman‍nāgatau andhakārau hauti| sūriyau vā atthaṅgatau hauti| kāḷapakkhau ca upausathau hauti| tibbau ca vanasaṇḍau hauti| mahā ca kāḷamaighau akālamaighau (syā॰) abbhuṭṭhitau hauti| aivarūpaipi caturaṅgasaman‍nāgatai andhakārai samantā yaujanaṃ  passati| natthi sau kuṭṭau kuḍḍau (sī॰) vā kavāṭaṃ vā pākārau vā pabbatau vā gacchau vā latā vā āvaraṇaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya| aikañ‍cai tilaphalaṃ nimittaṃ  katvā tilavāhai pakkhipaiyya| tañ‍ñaiva tilaphalaṃ uddharaiyya| aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ bhagavatau pākatikaṃ maṃsacakkhu| aivaṃ bhagavā maṃsacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu|

kathaṃ bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu? bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai| yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai; yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| ākaṅkhamānau ca bhagavā aikampi laukadhātuṃ passaiyya, dvaipi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, tissaupi laukadhātuyau  passaiyya, catassaupi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, pañ‍capi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, dasapi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, vīsampi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, tiṃsampi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, cattālīsampi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, pañ‍ñāsampi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, satampi laukadhātuyau passaiyya, sahassimpi cūḷanikaṃ laukadhātuṃ passaiyya, dvisahassimpi majjhimikaṃ laukadhātuṃ passaiyya, tisahassimpi laukadhātuṃ passaiyya, mahāsahassimpi tisahassiṃ mahāsahassimpi (sī॰ ka॰) laukadhātuṃ passaiyya| yāvatakaṃ vā pana ākaṅkhaiyya tāvatakaṃ passaiyya| aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ bhagavatau dibbacakkhu| aivaṃ bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu|

kathaṃ bhagavā pañ‍ñācakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu? bhagavā mahāpañ‍ñau puthupañ‍ñau hāsapañ‍ñau javanapañ‍ñau tikkhapañ‍ñau nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau pañ‍ñāpabhaidakusalau pabhin‍nañāṇau adhigatapaṭisambhidau catuvaisāraj‍jappattau dasabaladhārī purisāsabhau purisasīhau purisanāgau purisājañ‍ñau purisadhaurayhau anantañāṇau anantataijau anantayasau aḍḍhau mahaddhanau dhanavā naitā vinaitā anunaitā pañ‍ñāpaitā anijjhāpaitā paikkhaitā pasādaitā| sau hi bhagavā anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā, asañ‍jātassa maggassa sañ‍janaitā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggañ‍ñū maggavidū maggakauvidau maggānugā ca pana aitarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchā saman‍nāgatā|

sau  hi bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti  passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau  dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau| natthi tassa bhagavatau añ‍ñātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya| atītaṃ anāgataṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ (syā॰) mahāni॰ 69 upādāya sabbai dhammā sabbākāraina buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇamukhai āpāthaṃ āgacchanti| yaṃ kiñ‍ci naiyyaṃ nāma atthi jānitabbaṃ atthi dhammaṃ jānitabbaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) attatthau vā paratthau vā ubhayatthau vā diṭṭhadhammikau vā atthau  samparāyikau vā atthau uttānau vā atthau gambhīrau vā atthau gūḷhau vā atthau paṭicchan‍nau vā atthau naiyyau vā atthau nītau vā atthau anavaj‍jau vā atthau nik‍kilaisau vā atthau vaudānau vā atthau paramatthau vā atthau, sabbaṃ taṃ paramatthau vā, sabbantaṃ (syā॰) antaubuddhañāṇai parivattati|

sabbaṃ kāyakammaṃ buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇānuparivatti sabbaṃ vacīkammaṃ… sabbaṃ manaukammaṃ… atītai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, anāgatai pac‍cuppan‍nai appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ| yāvatakaṃ naiyyaṃ tāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ, yāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ tāvatakaṃ naiyyaṃ; naiyyapariyantikaṃ ñāṇaṃ ñāṇapariyantikaṃ naiyyaṃ, naiyyaṃ atik‍kamitvā ñāṇaṃ nappavattati, ñāṇaṃ atik‍kamitvā naiyyapathau natthi| añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyantaṭṭhāyinau tai dhammā| yathā dvin‍naṃ samuggapaṭalānaṃ sammāphusitānaṃ haiṭṭhimaṃ samuggapaṭalaṃ uparimaṃ nātivattati, uparimaṃ samuggapaṭalaṃ haiṭṭhimaṃ nātivattati, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyantaṭṭhāyinau; aivamaiva buddhassa  bhagavatau naiyyañ‍ca ñāṇañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyantaṭṭhāyinau| yāvatakaṃ naiyyaṃ tāvatakaṃ  ñāṇaṃ, yāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ tāvatakaṃ naiyyaṃ; naiyyapariyantikaṃ ñāṇaṃ ñāṇapariyantikaṃ naiyyaṃ| naiyyaṃ atik‍kamitvā ñāṇaṃ nappavattati, ñāṇaṃ atik‍kamitvā naiyyapathau natthi| añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyantaṭṭhāyinau tai dhammā|

sabbadhammaisu buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ pavattati| sabbai dhammā buddhassa bhagavatau āvaj‍janapaṭibaddhā ākaṅkhapaṭibaddhā manasikārapaṭibaddhā cittuppādapaṭibaddhā| sabbasattaisu buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ pavattati| sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ bhagavā āsayaṃ jānāti, anusayaṃ jānāti, caritaṃ jānāti, adhimuttiṃ jānāti, apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai bhabbābhabbai sattai pajānāti| sadaivakau laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā antaubuddhañāṇai parivattati parivattanti (ka॰) pasūrasuttaniddaisai avasānaipi|

yathā yai kaici macchakacchapā antamasau timitimiṅgalaṃ upādāya antaumahāsamuddai parivattanti; aivamaiva sadaivakau laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā  antaubuddhañāṇai parivattati parivattanti (ka॰) pasūrasuttaniddaisai avasānaipi| yathā yai kaici pakkhī antamasau garuḷaṃ vainataiyyaṃ upādāya ākāsassa padaisai parivattanti; aivamaiva yaipi tai  sāriputtasamā pañ‍ñāya taipi buddhañāṇassa padaisai parivattanti; buddhañāṇaṃ daivamanussānaṃ pañ‍ñaṃ pharitvā abhibhavitvā tiṭṭhati| yaipi tai khattiyapaṇḍitā brāhmaṇapaṇḍitā  gahapatipaṇḍitā samaṇapaṇḍitā nipuṇā kataparappavādā vālavaidhirūpā vaubhindantā mañ‍ñai caranti pañ‍ñāgataina diṭṭhigatāni, tai pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharitvā abhisaṅkharitvā tathāgataṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchanti gūḷhāni ca paṭicchan‍nāni ca| kathitā visaj‍jitā ca tai pañhā bhagavatā haunti niddiṭṭhakāraṇā| upakkhittakā ca  tai bhagavatau sampaj‍janti| atha khau bhagavā tattha atiraucati yadidaṃ pañ‍ñāyāti| aivaṃ bhagavā pañ‍ñācakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu|

kathaṃ bhagavā buddhacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu? bhagavā buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau addasa sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau viharantai| saiyyathāpi nāma uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakā anuggatāni antaunimuggapausīni, appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni samaudakaṃ ṭhitāni, appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakā ac‍cuggamma tiṭṭhanti anupalittāni udakaina; aivamaiva bhagavā buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau addasa sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau  viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau viharantai| jānāti bhagavā – ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau rāgacaritau, ayaṃ dausacaritau, ayaṃ mauhacaritau, ayaṃ vitak‍kacaritau, ayaṃ saddhācaritau, ayaṃ ñāṇacaritau’’ti| rāgacaritassa bhagavā puggalassa asubhakathaṃ kathaiti; dausacaritassa bhagavā puggalassa maittābhāvanaṃ ācikkhati; mauhacaritassa bhagavā puggalassa uddaisai paripucchāya kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina dhammasākacchāya garusaṃvāsai nivaisaiti; vitak‍kacaritassa bhagavā puggalassa ānāpānassatiṃ ācikkhati; saddhācaritassa bhagavā puggalassa pasādanīyaṃ nimittaṃ ācikkhati buddhasubaudhiṃ  buddhasubuddhataṃ (ka॰) dhammasudhammataṃ saṅghasuppaṭipattiṃ sīlāni ca; attanau ñāṇacaritassa bhagavā puggalassa ācikkhati vipassanānimittaṃ anic‍cākāraṃ dukkhākāraṃ anattākāraṃ|

‘‘sailai  yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau, yathāpi passai janataṃ samantatau|

tathūpamaṃ dhammamayaṃ sumaidha, pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu|

saukāvatiṇṇaṃ janatamapaitasaukau, avaikkhassu jātijarābhibhūta’’nti||

aivaṃ bhagavā buddhacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu|

kathaṃ bhagavā samantacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhu? samantacakkhu vuc‍cati sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ| bhagavā sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaina upaitau samupaitau upagatau  samupagatau upapan‍nau samupapan‍nau saman‍nāgatau|

‘‘na tassa adiṭṭhamidhatthi kiñ‍ci, athau aviñ‍ñātamajānitabbaṃ|

sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñāsi yadatthi naiyyaṃ, tathāgatau taina samantacakkhū’’ti||

aivaṃ bhagavā samantacakkhunāpi vivaṭacakkhūti – akittayi vivaṭacakkhu|

sakkhidhammaṃ parissayavinayanti| sakkhidhammanti na itihitihaṃ, na itikirāya, na paramparāya, na piṭakasampadāya, na tak‍kahaitu, na nayahaitu, na ākāraparivitak‍kaina, na diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā sāmaṃ sayamabhiñ‍ñātaṃ attapac‍cakkhaṃ dhammanti – sakkhidhammaṃ| parissayavinayanti| parissayāti dvai parissayā – pākaṭaparissayā ca paṭicchan‍naparissayā ca| katamai pākaṭaparissayā? sīhā byagghā dīpī acchā taracchā  kaukā mahiṃsā gaumahisā (syā॰) mahāni॰ 5 hatthī ahi vicchikā satapadī, caurā vā assu mānavā vā katakammā vā akatakammā vā, cakkhuraugau sautaraugau ghānaraugau jivhāraugau kāyaraugau sīsaraugau kaṇṇaraugau mukharaugau dantaraugau kāsau sāsau pināsau ḍāhau ḍahau (sī॰ syā॰) mahāni॰ 5 jarau kucchiraugau mucchā pakkhandikā sūlā visūcikā kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍau kilāsau sausau apamārau  daddu kaṇḍu kacchu rakhasā vitacchikā lauhitapittaṃ lauhitaṃ pittaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) madhumaihau aṃsā piḷakā bhagandalā, pittasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā saimhasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā  vātasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā san‍nipātikā ābādhā utupariṇāmajā ābādhā visamaparihārajā ābādhā aupak‍kamikā ābādhā kammavipākajā ābādhā, sītaṃ uṇhaṃ jighacchā pipāsā uc‍cārau passāvau ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassā iti vā – imai vuc‍canti pākaṭaparissayā|

katamai paṭicchan‍naparissayā? kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ  byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ rāgau dausau mauhau kaudhau upanāhau makkhau paḷāsau issā macchariyaṃ māyā sāṭhaiyyaṃ thambhau sārambhau mānau atimānau madau pamādau, sabbai kilaisā sabbai duc‍caritā sabbai darathā sabbai pariḷāhā sabbai santāpā sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārā – imai vuc‍canti paṭicchan‍naparissayā|

parissayāti kainaṭṭhaina parissayā? parisahantīti parissayā, parihānāya saṃvattantīti parissayā, tatrāsayāti parissayā| kathaṃ parisahantīti parissayā? tai parissayā taṃ puggalaṃ sahanti parisahanti abhibhavanti ajjhauttharanti pariyādiyanti maddanti| aivaṃ parisahantīti – parissayā|

kathaṃ parihānāya saṃvattantīti parissayā? tai parissayā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ antarāyāya parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamaisaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? sammāpaṭipadāya anulaumapaṭipadāya apac‍canīkapaṭipadāya  aviruddhapaṭipadāya anvatthapaṭipadāya dhammānudhammapaṭipadāya sīlaisu paripūrakāritāya indriyaisu guttadvāratāya bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutāya jāgariyānuyaugassa satisampajañ‍ñassa catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugassa catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa bhāvanānuyaugassa – imaisaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ antarāyāya parihānāya saṃvattanti| aivampi parihānāya saṃvattantīti – parissayā|

kathaṃ  tatrāsayāti parissayā? tatthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppaj‍janti attabhāvasan‍nissayā| yathā bilai bilāsayā pāṇā sayanti, dakai dakāsayā pāṇā sayanti, vanai vanāsayā pāṇā sayanti, rukkhai rukkhāsayā pāṇā sayanti; aivamaiva tatthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppaj‍janti attabhāvasan‍nissayāti| aivampi tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘sāntaivāsikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sācariyakau dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sāntaivāsikau sācariyakau dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā sañ‍ñaujaniyā, tyassa antau vasanti anvāssavanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā  sāntaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| tai naṃ samudācaranti, samudācaranti samudācārainti (sī॰) naṃ pāpakā  akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sācariyakauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā … manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā sañ‍ñaujaniyā, tyassa antau vasanti anvāssavanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sāntaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| tai naṃ samudācaranti, samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sācariyakauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sāntaivāsikau sācariyakau dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharatī’’ti| aivampi tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, antarāmalā antarāamittā antarāsapattā antarāvadhakā antarāpac‍catthikā| katamai tayau? laubhau, bhikkhavai, antarāmalau antarāmalaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) itivu॰ 88 antarāamittau antarāsapattau antarāvadhakau antarāpac‍catthikau; dausau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… mauhau, bhikkhavai, antarāmalau antarāamittau antarāsapattau antarāvadhakau antarāpac‍catthikau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau antarāmalā antarāamittā antarāsapattā antarāvadhakā antarāpac‍catthikā’’ti|

‘‘anatthajananau  laubhau, laubhau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhati||

‘‘luddhau  atthaṃ na jānāti, luddhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhantamaṃ andhatamaṃ (syā॰) itivu॰ 88 tadā hauti, yaṃ laubhau sahatai naraṃ||

‘‘anatthajananau dausau, dausau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhati||

‘‘kuddhau atthaṃ na jānāti, kuddhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhantamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ dausau sahatai naraṃ||

‘‘anatthajananau  mauhau, mauhau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhati||

‘‘mūḷhau atthaṃ na jānāti, mūḷhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhantamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ mauhau sahatai nara’’nti||

aivampi tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘tayau khau, mahārāja, purisassa dhammā ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍janti ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya| katamai tayau? laubhau khau, mahārāja, purisassa dhammau ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya; dausau khau, mahārāja…pai॰… mauhau khau, mahārāja, purisassa dhammau ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya| imai khau, mahārāja, tayau purisassa dhammā ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍janti ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya|

‘‘laubhau  dausau ca mauhau ca, purisaṃ pāpacaitasaṃ|

hiṃsanti attasambhūtā, tacasāraṃva samphala’’nti||

aivampi tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

vuttañhaitaṃ  bhagavatā –

‘‘rāgau ca dausau ca itau nidānā, aratī ratī laumahaṃsau itaujā|

itau samuṭṭhāya manauvitak‍kā, kumārakā dhaṅkamivaussajantī’’ti||

aivampi tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

parissayavinayanti  parissayavinayaṃ parissayappahānaṃ parissayavūpasamaṃ parissayapaṭinissaggaṃ parissayapaṭipassaddhiṃ amataṃ nibbānanti – sakkhidhammaṃ parissayavinayaṃ|

paṭipadaṃ vadaihi bhaddantaiti| paṭipadaṃ vadaihi – sammāpaṭipadaṃ anulaumapaṭipadaṃ apac‍canīkapaṭipadaṃ  aviruddhapaṭipadaṃ anvatthapaṭipadaṃ dhammānudhammapaṭipadaṃ sīlaisu paripūrakāritaṃ indriyaisu guttadvārataṃ bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutaṃ jāgariyānuyaugaṃ satisampajañ‍ñaṃ cattārau satipaṭṭhānai cattārau sammappadhānai cattārau iddhipādai pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgai ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ nibbānañ‍ca nibbānagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ vadaihi ācikkha daisaihi pañ‍ñapaihi paṭṭhapaihi vivara vibhaja uttānīkarauhi pakāsaihīti – paṭipadaṃ vadaihi| bhaddantaiti sau nimmitau  buddhaṃ bhagavantaṃ ālapati| atha vā yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ ācikkhasi daisaisi pañ‍ñapaisi paṭṭhapaisi vivari vibhaji uttānīakāsi pakāsaisi sabbaṃ taṃ sundaraṃ bhaddakaṃ kalyāṇaṃ anavaj‍jaṃ saivitabbanti – paṭipadaṃ vadaihi bhaddantai|

pātimaukkhaṃ atha vāpi samādhinti| pātimaukkhanti sīlaṃ patiṭṭhā ādi caraṇaṃ saṃyamau saṃvarau mukhaṃ pamukhaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| atha vāpi samādhinti yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhatamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhīti – pātimaukkhaṃ atha vāpi samādhiṃ|

taināha sau nimmitau –

‘‘akittayī vivaṭacakkhu, sakkhidhammaṃ parissayavinayaṃ|

paṭipadaṃ vadaihi bhaddantai, pātimaukkhaṃ atha vāpi samādhi’’nti||

157.

 cakkhūhi  naiva laulassa, gāmakathāya āvarayai sautaṃ|

rasai ca nānugijjhaiyya, na ca mamāyaitha kiñ‍ci laukasmiṃ||

cakkhūhi  naiva laulassāti| kathaṃ cakkhulaulauti? idhaikac‍cau cakkhulauliyaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘‘adiṭṭhaṃ dakkhitabbaṃ, diṭṭhaṃ samatik‍kamitabba’’nti ārāmaina ārāmaṃ uyyānaina uyyānaṃ gāmaina gāmaṃ nigamaina nigamaṃ nagaraina nagaraṃ raṭṭhaina raṭṭhaṃ janapadaina janapadaṃ dīghacārikaṃ anavaṭṭhitacārikaṃ anavatthitacārikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) anuyuttau ca hauti rūpassa dassanāya| aivampi cakkhulaulau hauti|

atha vā bhikkhu antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau vīthiṃ paṭipan‍nau asaṃvutau gacchati hatthiṃ aulaukaintau, assaṃ aulaukaintau, rathaṃ aulaukaintau, pattiṃ aulaukaintau, itthiyau aulaukaintau, purisai aulaukaintau, kumārakai aulaukaintau, kumārikāyau aulaukaintau, antarāpaṇaṃ aulaukaintau, gharamukhāni aulaukaintau, uddhaṃ aulaukaintau, adhau aulaukaintau, disāvidisaṃ vipaikkhamānau gacchati| aivampi cakkhulaulau hauti|

atha vā bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā nimittaggāhī hauti anubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipaj‍jati, na rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivampi cakkhulaulau hauti|

yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ visūkadassanaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – nac‍caṃ gītaṃ vāditaṃ paikkhaṃ akkhānaṃ pāṇissaraṃ vaitāḷaṃ kumbhathūṇaṃ kumbhathūnaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) saubhanakaṃ saubhanagarakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) caṇḍālaṃ vaṃsaṃ dhauvanaṃ hatthiyuddhaṃ assayuddhaṃ mahiṃsayuddhaṃ mahisayuddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) usabhayuddhaṃ ajayuddhaṃ maiṇḍayuddhaṃ kuk‍kuṭayuddhaṃ vaṭṭakayuddhaṃ daṇḍayuddhaṃ muṭṭhiyuddhaṃ nibbuddhaṃ uyyaudhikaṃ  balaggaṃ sainābyūhaṃ anīkadassanaṃ iti vā| aivampi cakkhulaulau hauti|

kathaṃ  na cakkhulaulau hauti? idha bhikkhu antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau vīthiṃ paṭipan‍nau saṃvutau gacchati na hatthiṃ aulaukaintau, na assaṃ aulaukaintau, na rathaṃ aulaukaintau, na pattiṃ aulaukaintau, na itthiyau aulaukaintau, na purisai aulaukaintau, na kumārakai aulaukaintau, na kumārikāyau aulaukaintau, na antarāpaṇaṃ aulaukaintau, na gharamukhāni aulaukaintau, na uddhaṃ aulaukaintau, na adhau aulaukaintau, na disāvidisāvipaikkhamānau gacchati| aivampi na cakkhulaulau hauti|

atha vā bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivampi na cakkhulaulau hauti|

yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ visūkadassanaṃ ananuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – nac‍caṃ gītaṃ vāditaṃ paikkhaṃ akkhānaṃ…pai॰… anīkadassanaṃ iti vā| aivarūpā visūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti| aivampi na cakkhulaulau hauti|

cakkhūhi  naiva laulassāti| cakkhulauliyaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, cakkhulauliyā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā  viharaiyyāti – cakkhūhi naiva laulassa|

gāmakathāya āvarayai sautanti| gāmakathā vuc‍cati bāttiṃsa tiracchānakathā, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathā caurakathā mahāmattakathā sainākathā bhayakathā yuddhakathā an‍nakathā pānakathā vatthakathā yānakathā sayanakathā mālākathā gandhakathā ñātikathā gāmakathā nigamakathā  nagarakathā janapadakathā itthikathā itthikathā purisakathā (bahūsu) sūrakathā visikhākathā kumbhaṭṭhānakathā pubbapaitakathā nānattakathā laukakkhāyikā samuddakkhāyikā itibhavābhavakathā iti vā| gāmakathāya āvarayai sautanti| gāmakathāya sautaṃ āvaraiyya nivāraiyya saṃvaraiyya rakkhaiyya gaupaiyya pidahaiyya pacchindaiyyāti – gāmakathāya āvarayai sautaṃ|

rasai ca nānugijjhaiyyāti| rasai cāti mūlarasau khandharasau tacarasau pattarasau puppharasau phalarasau ambilaṃ madhuraṃ tittakaṃ kaṭukaṃ lauṇikaṃ khārikaṃ lambikaṃ kasāvau sādu asādu sītaṃ uṇhaṃ| santaikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā rasagiddhā; tai jivhaggaina rasaggāni pariyaisantā āhiṇḍanti, tai ambilaṃ labhitvā anambilaṃ pariyaisanti, anambilaṃ labhitvā ambilaṃ pariyaisanti…pai॰… sītaṃ labhitvā uṇhaṃ pariyaisanti, uṇhaṃ labhitvā sītaṃ pariyaisanti| tai yaṃ yaṃ labhitvā taina taina na tussanti, aparāparaṃ pariyaisanti, manāpikaisu rasaisu rattā giddhā gadhitā mucchitā ajjhausan‍nā laggā laggitā palibuddhā| yassaisā rasataṇhā pahīnā samucchin‍nā…pai॰… ñāṇagginā  daḍḍhā, sau paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti naiva davāya…pai॰… anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cāti|

yathā vaṇaṃ ālimpaiyya yāvadaiva āruhaṇatthāya āruhanatthāya (ka॰) purābhaidasuttaniddaisai sattamagāthāvaṇṇanāyaṃ, yathā vā pana akkhaṃ abbhañ‍jaiyya yāvadaiva bhārassa nittharaṇatthāya, yathā vā puttamaṃsaṃ āhāraṃ āhāraiyya yāvadaiva kantārassa nittharaṇatthāya; aivamaivaṃ bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ  āhāraiti naiva davāya…pai॰… anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cāti rasataṇhaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, rasataṇhāya āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – rasai ca nānugijjhaiyya|

na ca mamāyaitha kiñ‍ci laukasminti| mamattāti dvai mamattā – taṇhāmamattañ‍ca diṭṭhimamattañ‍ca  …pai॰… idaṃ taṇhāmamattaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ diṭṭhimamattaṃ| taṇhāmamattaṃ pahāya diṭṭhimamattaṃ paṭinissaj‍jitvā cakkhuṃ na mamāyaiyya na gaṇhaiyya na parāmasaiyya nābhinivisaiyya; sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… kulaṃ… gaṇaṃ… āvāsaṃ… lābhaṃ… yasaṃ… pasaṃsaṃ… sukhaṃ… cīvaraṃ… piṇḍapātaṃ… saināsanaṃ… gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ… kāmadhātuṃ… rūpadhātuṃ… arūpadhātuṃ… kāmabhavaṃ… rūpabhavaṃ… arūpabhavaṃ… sañ‍ñābhavaṃ… asañ‍ñābhavaṃ… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñābhavaṃ … aikavaukārabhavaṃ… catuvaukārabhavaṃ… pañ‍cavaukārabhavaṃ … atītaṃ… anāgataṃ… pac‍cuppan‍naṃ… diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai na mamāyaiyya na gaṇhaiyya na parāmasaiyya nābhinivisaiyya| kiñ‍cīti kiñ‍ci rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ| laukasminti apāyalaukai…pai॰… āyatanalaukaiti – na ca mamāyaitha kiñ‍ci laukasmiṃ|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘cakkhūhi naiva laulassa, gāmakathāya āvarayai sautaṃ|

rasai ca nānugijjhaiyya, na ca mamāyaitha kiñ‍ci laukasmi’’nti||

158.

 phassaina yadā phuṭṭhassa, paridaivaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci|

bhavañ‍ca nābhijappaiyya, bhairavaisu ca na sampavaidhaiyya|| 

phassaina  yadā phuṭṭhassāti| phassauti raugaphassau| raugaphassaina phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau assa; cakkhuraugaina phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau assa, sautaraugaina…pai॰… ghānaraugaina… jivhāraugaina… kāyaraugaina… sīsaraugaina… kaṇṇaraugaina… mukharaugaina… dantaraugaina… kāsaina… sāsaina… pināsaina… ḍāhaina… jaraina… kucchiraugaina… mucchāya… pakkhandikāya… sūlāya… visūcikāya… kuṭṭhaina… gaṇḍaina… kilāsaina… sausaina… apamāraina… dadduyā… kaṇḍuyā … kacchuyā… rakhasāya… vitacchikāya… lauhitaina… pittaina… madhumaihaina… aṃsāya… piḷakāya… bhagandalaina… pittasamuṭṭhānaina ābādhaina… saimhasamuṭṭhānaina ābādhaina… vātasamuṭṭhānaina ābādhaina… san‍nipātikaina ābādhaina… utupariṇāmajaina ābādhaina… visamaparihārajaina ābādhaina… aupak‍kamikaina ābādhaina… kammavipākajaina ābādhaina… sītaina… uṇhaina… jighacchāya… pipāsāya… uc‍cāraina… passāvaina… ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassaihi phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau assāti – phassaina yadā phuṭṭhassa|

paridaivaṃ  bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍cīti| ādaivaṃ paridaivaṃ ādaivanaṃ paridaivanaṃ ādaivitattaṃ paridaivitattaṃ vācā palāpaṃ vippalāpaṃ lālappaṃ lālappāyanaṃ lālappāyitattaṃ na karaiyya na janaiyya na sañ‍janaiyya na  nibbattaiyya nābhinibbattaiyya| kuhiñ‍cīti kuhiñ‍ci kimhici katthaci ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā ajjhattabahiddhā vāti – paridaivaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci|

bhavañ‍ca nābhijappaiyyāti| kāmabhavaṃ na jappaiyya, rūpabhavaṃ na jappaiyya, arūpabhavaṃ na jappaiyya na pajappaiyya nābhijappaiyyāti – bhavañ‍ca nābhijappaiyya|

bhairavaisu ca na sampavaidhaiyyāti| bhairavāti aikainākāraina bhayampi bhairavampi tañ‍ñaiva| vuttañhaitaṃ  bhagavatā – ‘‘aitaṃ nūna taṃ bhayaṃ bhairavaṃ na jahai āgacchatī’’ti| bahiddhārammaṇaṃ vuttaṃ sīhā byagghā  dīpī acchā taracchā kaukā mahiṃsā assā hatthī ahi vicchikā satapadī, caurā vā assu mānavā vā katakammā vā akatakammā vā| athāparaina ākāraina bhayaṃ vuc‍cati ajjhattikaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhayaṃ bhayānakaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau caitasau ubbaigau utrāsau, jātibhayaṃ jarābhayaṃ byādhibhayaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ rājabhayaṃ caurabhayaṃ aggibhayaṃ udakabhayaṃ attānuvādabhayaṃ parānuvādabhayaṃ daṇḍabhayaṃ duggatibhayaṃ ūmibhayaṃ kumbhīlabhayaṃ āvaṭṭabhayaṃ susukābhayaṃ suṃsukābhayaṃ (syā॰) ājīvikabhayaṃ asilaukabhayaṃ parisāya sāraj‍jabhayaṃ madanabhayaṃ duggatibhayaṃ bhayaṃ bhayānakaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau caitasau ubbaigau utrāsau| bhairavaisu ca na sampavaidhaiyyāti bhairavai passitvā vā suṇitvā vā na vaidhaiyya na pavaidhaiyya na sampavaidhaiyya na tasaiyya na uttasaiyya na paritasaiyya na bhāyaiyya na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, abhīrū assa achambhī anutrāsī apalāyī, pahīnabhayabhairavau vigatalaumahaṃsau viharaiyyāti – bhairavaisu ca na sampavaidhaiyya|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘phassaina yadā phuṭṭhassa, paridaivaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci|

bhavañ‍ca nābhijappaiyya, bhairavaisu ca na sampavaidhaiyyā’’ti||

159.

 an‍nānamathau pānānaṃ, khādanīyānamathaupi vatthānaṃ| 

laddhā  na san‍nidhiṃ kayirā, na ca parittasai tāni alabhamānau||

an‍nānamathau  pānānaṃ, khādanīyānamathaupi vatthānanti| an‍nānanti audanau kummāsau sattu macchau maṃsaṃ| pānānanti aṭṭha pānāni – ambapānaṃ, jambupānaṃ, caucapānaṃ, maucapānaṃ, madhupānaṃ, muddikapānaṃ, sālukapānaṃ, phārusakapānaṃ| aparānipi aṭṭha pānāni – kausambapānaṃ, kaulapānaṃ, badarapānaṃ, ghatapānaṃ, tailapānaṃ, payaupānaṃ, yāgupānaṃ, rasapānaṃ| khādanīyānanti piṭṭhakhaj‍jakaṃ, pūvakhaj‍jakaṃ, mūlakhaj‍jakaṃ, tacakhaj‍jakaṃ, pattakhaj‍jakaṃ, pupphakhaj‍jakaṃ, phalakhaj‍jakaṃ| vatthānanti cha cīvarāni – khaumaṃ, kappāsikaṃ, kausaiyyaṃ, kambalaṃ, sāṇaṃ, bhaṅganti – an‍nānamathau pānānaṃ khādanīyānamathaupi vatthānaṃ|

laddhā na san‍nidhiṃ kayirāti| laddhāti laddhā labhitvā adhigantvā vinditvā paṭilabhitvā na kuhanāya, na lapanāya, na naimittikatāya, na nippaisikatāya, na lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīsanatāya nijigiṃsanatāya (sī॰ syā॰), na kaṭṭhadānaina, na vaiḷudānaina, na pattadānaina, na pupphadānaina, na phaladānaina, na sinānadānaina, na cuṇṇadānaina, na mattikādānaina, na dantakaṭṭhadānaina, na mukhaudakadānaina, na cāṭukamyatāya, na muggasūpyatāya na muggasuppatāya (sī॰), na muggasūpatāya (syā॰), na pāribhaṭayayatāya, na pīṭhamaddikatāya piṭṭhimaṃsikatāya (sī॰), piṭṭhimaddikatāya (ka॰), na vatthuvij‍jāya, na tiracchānavij‍jāya, na aṅgavij‍jāya, na nakkhattavij‍jāya, na dūtagamanaina, na pahiṇagamanaina, na jaṅghapaisaniyaina, na vaij‍jakammaina, na navakammaina, na piṇḍapaṭipiṇḍakaina, na  dānānuppadānaina dhammaina samaina laddhā labhitvā adhigantvā vinditvā paṭilabhitvāti – laddhā| na san‍nidhiṃ kayirāti an‍nasan‍nidhiṃ pānasan‍nidhiṃ vatthasan‍nidhiṃ yānasan‍nidhiṃ sayanasan‍nidhiṃ gandhasan‍nidhiṃ āmisasan‍nidhiṃ na karaiyya na janaiyya na sañ‍janaiyya na nibbattaiyya nābhinibbattaiyyāti – laddhā na san‍nidhiṃ kayirā|

na ca parittasai tāni alabhamānauti| an‍naṃ vā na labhāmi, pānaṃ vā na labhāmi, vatthaṃ vā na labhāmi, kulaṃ vā na labhāmi, gaṇaṃ vā na labhāmi, āvāsaṃ vā na labhāmi, lābhaṃ vā na labhāmi, yasaṃ vā na labhāmi, pasaṃsaṃ vā na labhāmi, sukhaṃ vā na labhāmi, cīvaraṃ vā na labhāmi, piṇḍapātaṃ vā na labhāmi, saināsanaṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānupaṭṭhākaṃ vā na labhāmi, ‘‘appañ‍ñātaumhī’’ti na tasaiyya na uttasaiyya na parittasaiyya na bhāyaiyya na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, abhīrū assa achambhī anutrāsī apalāyī, pahīnabhayabhairavau vigatalaumahaṃsau viharaiyyāti – na ca parittasai tāni alabhamānau|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘an‍nānamathau pānānaṃ, khādanīyānamathaupi vatthānaṃ|

laddhā na san‍nidhiṃ kayirā, na ca parittasai tāni alabhamānau’’ti||

160.

 jhāyī  na pādalaulassa, viramai kuk‍kuc‍cā nappamaj‍jaiyya| 

athāsanaisu sayanaisu, appasaddaisu bhikkhu viharaiyya|| 

jhāyī na pādalaulassāti| jhāyīti paṭhamainapi jhānaina jhāyī, dutiyainapi jhānaina jhāyī, tatiyainapi jhānaina jhāyī, catutthainapi jhānaina jhāyī, savitak‍kasavicārainapi jhānaina jhāyī, avitak‍kavicāramattainapi jhānaina jhāyī, avitak‍kaavicārainapi jhānaina jhāyī, sappītikainapi jhānaina jhāyī, nippītikainapi jhānaina jhāyī, pītisahagatainapi jhānaina jhāyī, sātasahagatainapi jhānaina jhāyī, sukhasahagatainapi jhānaina jhāyī, upaikkhāsahagatainapi jhānaina jhāyī, suñ‍ñatainapi jhānaina jhāyī, animittainapi jhānaina jhāyī, appaṇihitainapi jhānaina jhāyī, laukiyainapi jhānaina jhāyī, laukuttarainapi jhānaina jhāyī, jhānaratau aikattamanuyuttau paramatthagarukauti sadatthagarukauti (sī॰ syā॰) – jhāyī|

na  pādalaulassāti| kathaṃ pādalaulau hauti? idhaikac‍cau pādalauliyaina saman‍nāgatau hauti ārāmaina ārāmaṃ uyyānaina uyyānaṃ gāmaina gāmaṃ nigamaina nigamaṃ nagaraina nagaraṃ raṭṭhaina raṭṭhaṃ janapadaina janapadaṃ dīghacārikaṃ anavaṭṭhitacārikaṃ anuyuttau viharati| aivampi pādalaulau hauti|

atha vā bhikkhu antaupisaṅghārāmai pādalauliyaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| na atthahaitu na kāraṇahaitu uddhatau avūpasantacittau  parivaiṇatau parivaiṇaṃ gacchati vihāratau vihāraṃ gacchati aḍḍhayaugatau  aḍḍhayaugaṃ gacchati pāsādatau pāsādaṃ gacchati hammiyatau hammiyaṃ gacchati guhāya guhaṃ gacchati laiṇatau laiṇaṃ gacchati kuṭitau kuṭiṃ gacchati kūṭāgāratau kūṭāgāraṃ gacchati aṭṭatau aṭṭaṃ gacchati māḷatau māḷaṃ gacchati uddaṇḍatau uddaṇḍaṃ gacchati upaṭṭhānasālatau upaṭṭhānasālaṃ gacchati maṇḍalamāḷatau maṇḍalamāḷaṃ gacchati rukkhamūlatau rukkhamūlaṃ gacchati| yattha vā pana bhikkhū nisīdanti tahiṃ gacchati, tattha aikassa vā dutiyau hauti, dvin‍naṃ vā tatiyau hauti, tiṇṇaṃ vā catutthau hauti| tattha bahuṃ samphappalāpaṃ palapati, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ yānakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ iti bhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā| aivampi pādalaulau hauti|

na  pādalaulassāti| pādalauliyaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, pādalauliyā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau  nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyya caraiyya vicaraiyya iriyaiyya vattaiyya pālaiyya yapaiyya yāpaiyya, paṭisal‍lānārāmau assa paṭisal‍lānaratau ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau  anirākatajjhānau vipassanā saman‍nāgatau brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ jhāyī jhānaratau aikattamanuyuttau paramatthagarukauti – jhāyī na pādalaulassa|

viramai kuk‍kuc‍cā nappamaj‍jaiyyāti| kuk‍kuc‍canti hatthakuk‍kuc‍campi kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, pādakuk‍kuc‍campi kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, hatthapādakuk‍kuc‍campi kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñitā, kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñitā, avaj‍jai vaj‍jasañ‍ñitā, vaj‍jai avaj‍jasañ‍ñitā, yaṃ aivarūpaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanā kuk‍kuc‍cāyitattaṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ|

api ca dvīhi kāraṇaihi uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau – katattā ca akatattā ca| kathaṃ katattā ca akatattā ca uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau? ‘‘kataṃ mai kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, akataṃ mai kāyasucarita’’nti uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ caitasau  vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau, ‘‘kataṃ mai vacīduc‍caritaṃ, akataṃ mai vacīsucarita’’nti uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau, ‘‘kataṃ mai manauduc‍caritaṃ, akataṃ mai manausucaritanti…pai॰… ‘‘katau mai pāṇātipātau, akatā mai pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī’’ti uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ…pai॰… manauvilaikhau, ‘‘kataṃ mai adin‍nādānaṃ… katau mai kāmaisumicchācārau… katau mai musāvādau… katā mai pisuṇavācā… katā mai pharusavācā… katau mai samphappalāpau… katā mai abhijjhā… katau mai byāpādau… katā mai micchādiṭṭhi, akatā mai sammādiṭṭhī’’ti uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau| aivaṃ katattā ca akatattā ca uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau|

atha vā ‘‘sīlaisumhi  na paripūrakārī’’ti uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau, ‘‘indriyaisumhi aguttadvārau’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘bhaujanai amattañ‍ñumhī’’ti… ‘‘jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttaumhī’’ti… ‘‘na satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgataumhī’’ti… ‘‘abhāvitā mai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti… ‘‘abhāvitā mai cattārau sammappadhānā’’ti… ‘‘abhāvitā mai cattārau  iddhipādā’’ti… ‘‘abhāvitāni mai pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti… ‘‘abhāvitāni mai pañ‍ca balānī’’ti… ‘‘abhāvitā mai satta baujjhaṅgā’’ti… ‘‘abhāvitau mai ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’ti… ‘‘dukkhaṃ mai apariñ‍ñāta’’nti… ‘‘samudayau mai appahīnau’’ti… ‘‘maggau mai abhāvitau’’ti… ‘‘niraudhau mai asacchikatau’’ti uppaj‍jati kuk‍kuc‍caṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhau| viramai kuk‍kuc‍cāti kuk‍kuc‍cā āramaiyya viramaiyya paṭiviramaiyya kuk‍kuc‍caṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya| kuk‍kuc‍cā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – viramai kuk‍kuc‍cā|

nappamaj‍jaiyyāti sak‍kac‍cakārī assa sātac‍cakārī aṭṭhitakārī anaulīnavuttikau anikkhittacchandau anikkhittadhurau appamādau kusalaisu dhammaisu| ‘‘kadāhaṃ aparipūraṃ vā sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūraiyyaṃ, paripūraṃ vā sīlakkhandhaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggaṇhaiyya’’nti? yau tattha chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca thāmau ca appaṭivānī ca sati  ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca ātappaṃ padhānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ anuyaugau appamādau kusalaisu dhammaisu| ‘‘kadāhaṃ aparipūraṃ vā samādhikkhandhaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ… vimuttikkhandhaṃ… vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ… kadāhaṃ apariñ‍ñātaṃ vā dukkhaṃ parijānaiyyaṃ, appahīnai vā kilaisai pajahaiyyaṃ, abhāvitaṃ vā maggaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ, asacchikataṃ vā niraudhaṃ sacchikaraiyya’’nti? yau tattha chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca thāmau ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca ātappaṃ  padhānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ anuyaugau appamādau kusalaisu dhammaisūti – viramai kuk‍kuc‍cā nappamaj‍jaiyya|

athāsanaisu sayanaisu, appasaddaisu bhikkhu viharaiyyāti| athāti padasandhi…pai॰… āsanaṃ vuc‍cati yattha nisīdati – mañ‍cau pīṭhaṃ bhisi taṭṭikā cammakhaṇḍau tiṇasanthārau paṇṇasanthārau palālasanthārau| sayanaṃ vuc‍cati saināsanaṃ vihārau aḍḍhayaugau pāsādau hammiyaṃ guhāti – athāsanaisu sayanaisu|

appasaddaisu bhikkhu viharaiyyāti| appasaddaisu appanigghausaisu vijanavātaisu manussarāhassaiyyakaisu paṭisal‍lānasāruppaisu saināsanaisu caraiyya vicaraiyya viharaiyya iriyaiyya vattaiyya pālaiyya yapaiyya yāpaiyyāti – athāsanaisu sayanaisu appasaddaisu bhikkhu viharaiyya|

taināha  bhagavā –

‘‘jhāyī na pādalaulassa, viramai kuk‍kuc‍cā nappamaj‍jaiyya|

athāsanaisu  sayanaisu, appasaddaisu bhikkhu viharaiyyā’’ti||

161.

 niddaṃ na bahulīkaraiyya, jāgariyaṃ bhajaiyya ātāpī| 

tandiṃ māyaṃ hassaṃ khiḍḍaṃ, maithunaṃ vippajahai savibhūsaṃ|| 

niddaṃ na bahulīkaraiyyāti| rattindivaṃ chakauṭṭhāsaṃ kāraitvā pañ‍cakauṭṭhāsaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya aikakauṭṭhāsaṃ nippaj‍jaiyyāti – niddaṃ na bahulīkaraiyya|

jāgariyaṃ bhajaiyya ātāpīti| idha bhikkhu divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiyya, rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiyya, rattiyā majjhimaṃ  yāmaṃ  dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiyya pādaipādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasikaritvā, rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiyya|

jāgariyaṃ bhajaiyyāti jāgariyaṃ bhajaiyya sambhajaiyya saivaiyya nisaivaiyya saṃsaivaiyya paṭisaivaiyyāti – jāgariyaṃ bhajaiyya|

ātāpīti| ātappaṃ vuc‍cati vīriyaṃ viriyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)| yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau ṭhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittacchandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggahau vīriyaṃ  vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau| iminā ātāpaina upaitau samupaitau upagatau samupagatau upapan‍nau samupapan‍nau saman‍nāgatau sau vuc‍cati ātāpīti – jāgariyaṃ bhajaiyya ātāpī|

tandiṃ māyaṃ hassaṃ khiḍḍaṃ, maithunaṃ vippajahai savibhūsanti| tandīti tandinti (sī॰) tandī tandiyanā tandiyitattaṃ natthi attadaṇḍasuttaniddaisai khuddakavatthuvibhaṅgaipi tandimanakatā ālasyaṃ ālasyāyanā ālasyāyitattaṃ| māyā vuc‍cati vañ‍canikā cariyā| idhaikac‍cau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā…pai॰… vācāya manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā tassa paṭicchādanahaitu pāpikaṃ icchaṃ paṇidahati| mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti jañ‍ñūti (ka॰) icchati, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti saṅkappaiti, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti vācaṃ bhāsati, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti kāyaina parak‍kamati| yā aivarūpā  māyāvitā ac‍casarā vañ‍canā nikati nikiraṇā pariharaṇā gūhanā parigūhanā chādanā paricchādanā paṭicchādanā (sī॰ ka॰) mahāniddaisai anuttānīkammaṃ anāvikammaṃ vaucchādanā pāpakiriyā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati māyā| hassanti idhaikac‍cau ativailaṃ dantavidaṃsakaṃ hasati| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘kumārakamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai yadidaṃ ativailaṃ dantavidaṃsakaṃ hasita’’nti|

khiḍḍāti dvai khiḍḍā – kāyikā ca khiḍḍā vācasikā ca khiḍḍā| katamā kāyikā khiḍḍā? hatthīhipi kīḷanti, assaihipi kīḷanti, rathaihipi kīḷanti, dhanūhipi kīḷanti, aṭṭhapadaihipi kīḷanti, dasapadaihipi kīḷanti, ākāsaipi kīḷanti, parihārapathaipi kīḷanti, santikāyapi kīḷanti, khalikāyapi kīḷanti, ghaṭikāyapi kīḷanti, salākahatthainapi kīḷanti, akkhainapi kīḷanti, paṅkacīrainapi kīḷanti, vaṅkakainapi kīḷanti, maukkhacikāyapi kīḷanti, ciṅgulakainapi kīḷanti, pattāḷhakainapi kīḷanti, rathakainapi kīḷanti, dhanukainapi kīḷanti, akkharikāyapi kīḷanti, manaisikāyapi kīḷanti, yathāvaj‍jainapi kīḷanti – ayaṃ kāyikā khiḍḍā| katamā vācasikā khiḍḍā? mukhabhairikaṃ mukhabhairiyaṃ (syā॰) mukhālambaraṃ mukhaḍiṇḍimakaṃ mukhadaiṇḍimakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) mukhavalimakaṃ mukhabhairuḷakaṃ mukhabhairulakaṃ (sī॰) mukhadaddarikaṃ nāṭakaṃ lāpaṃ gītaṃ davakammaṃ – ayaṃ vācasikā khiḍḍā|

maithunadhammau nāma yau sau asaddhammau gāmadhammau vasaladhammau duṭṭhul‍lau audakantikau rahassau dvayaṃdvayasamāpatti| kiṃkāraṇā vuc‍cati maithunadhammau? ubhin‍naṃ rattānaṃ sārattānaṃ avassutānaṃ pariyuṭṭhitānaṃ pariyādin‍nacittānaṃ ubhin‍naṃ sadisānaṃ dhammauti, taṃkāraṇā vuc‍cati maithunadhammau| yathā ubhau kalahakārakā maithunakāti vuc‍canti, ubhau bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… ubhau bhassakārakā… ubhau adhikaraṇakārakā… ubhau vivādakārakā… ubhau vādinau… ubhau sal‍lāpakā maithunakāti vuc‍canti; aivamaiva ubhin‍naṃ rattānaṃ sārattānaṃ avassutānaṃ pariyuṭṭhitānaṃ pariyādin‍nacittānaṃ ubhin‍naṃ sadisānaṃ dhammauti, taṃkāraṇā vuc‍cati maithunadhammau|

vibhūsāti dvai vibhūsā – atthi agāriyassa vibhūsā, atthi pabbajitassa vibhūsā| katamā agāriyassa vibhūsā? kaisā ca massu ca mālā ca gandhā  ca vilaipanā ca ābharaṇā ca pilandhanā ca vatthañ‍ca sayanāsanañ‍ca vaiṭhanañ‍ca ucchādanaṃ parimaddanaṃ nhāpanaṃ sambāhanaṃ ādāsaṃ añ‍janaṃ mālāvilaipanaṃ mukhacuṇṇakaṃ mukhalaipaṃ hatthabandhaṃ sikhābandhaṃ vasikkhābandhaṃ (syā॰) daṇḍanāḷiyaṃ khaggaṃ chattaṃ citrā upāhanā uṇhīsaṃ maṇiṃ vāḷabījaniṃ audātāni vatthāni dīghadasāni iti vā – ayaṃ agāriyassa vibhūsā| katamā pabbajitassa vibhūsā? cīvaramaṇḍanā  pattamaṇḍanā saināsanamaṇḍanā imassa vā pūtikāyassa bāhirānaṃ vā parikkhārānaṃ maṇḍanā vibhūsanā kaiḷanā parikaiḷanā gaidhitatā gaidhitattaṃ capalatā cāpalyaṃ – ayaṃ pabbajitassa vibhūsā|

tandiṃ māyaṃ hassaṃ khiḍḍaṃ, maithunaṃ vippajahai savibhūsanti| tandiñ‍ca māyañ‍ca hassañ‍ca khiḍḍañ‍ca maithunadhammañ‍ca savibhūsaṃ saparivāraṃ saparibhaṇḍaṃ saparikkhāraṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyyāti – tandiṃ māyaṃ hassaṃ khiḍḍaṃ, maithunaṃ vippajahai savibhūsaṃ|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘niddaṃ  na bahulīkaraiyya, jāgariyaṃ bhajaiyya ātāpī|

tandiṃ māyaṃ hassaṃ khiḍḍaṃ, maithunaṃ vippajahai savibhūsa’’nti||

162.

 āthabbaṇaṃ  supinaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ, nau vidahai athaupi nakkhattaṃ|

virutañ‍ca  gabbhakaraṇaṃ, tikicchaṃ māmakau na saivaiyya|| 

āthabbaṇaṃ supinaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ, nau vidahai athaupi nakkhattanti| āthabbaṇikā āthabbaṇaṃ payaujainti, nagarai vā ruddhai rundhai (ka॰) saṅgāmai vā pac‍cupaṭṭhitai parasainapac‍catthikaisu pac‍cāmittaisu ītiṃ uppādainti, upaddavaṃ uppādainti, raugaṃ uppādainti, paj‍jarakaṃ karaunti, sūlaṃ karaunti, visūcikaṃ karaunti, pakkhandikaṃ karaunti| aivaṃ āthabbaṇikā āthabbaṇaṃ payaujainti|

supinapāṭhakā supinaṃ ādisanti, yau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ supinaṃ passati, aivaṃ vipākau hauti| yau majjhanhikasamayaṃ majjhantikasamayaṃ (bahūsu) supinaṃ passati, aivaṃ vipākau hauti| yau sāyanhasamayaṃ supinaṃ passati, aivaṃ vipākau hauti| yau purimai yāmai…pai॰… yau majjhimai yāmai… yau pacchimai yāmai… yau dakkhiṇaina passaina nipan‍nau… yau vāmaina passaina nipan‍nau… yau uttānaṃ nipan‍nau… yau avakuj‍ja nipan‍nau… yau candaṃ passati… yau sūriyaṃ passati… yau mahāsamuddaṃ passati… yau sinairuṃ pabbatarājānaṃ passati… yau hatthiṃ passati… yau assaṃ passati… yau rathaṃ passati… yau pattiṃ passati… yau sainābyūhaṃ passati… yau ārāmarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ passati… yau vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ passati… yau bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ passati… yau paukkharaṇīrāmaṇaiyyakaṃ paukkharaṇirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ (syā॰) passati, aivaṃ vipākau hautīti| aivaṃ supinapāṭhakā supinaṃ ādisanti|

lakkhaṇapāṭhakā  lakkhaṇaṃ ādisanti – maṇilakkhaṇaṃ daṇḍalakkhaṇaṃ  vatthalakkhaṇaṃ asilakkhaṇaṃ usulakkhaṇaṃ dhanulakkhaṇaṃ āvudhalakkhaṇaṃ itthilakkhaṇaṃ purisalakkhaṇaṃ kumārikālakkhaṇaṃ kumāralakkhaṇaṃ dāsilakkhaṇaṃ dāsalakkhaṇaṃ hatthilakkhaṇaṃ assalakkhaṇaṃ mahiṃsalakkhaṇaṃ usabhalakkhaṇaṃ gauṇalakkhaṇaṃ  ajalakkhaṇaṃ maiṇḍalakkhaṇaṃ  kuk‍kuṭalakkhaṇaṃ vaṭṭalakkhaṇaṃ gaudhālakkhaṇaṃ kaṇṇikālakkhaṇaṃ kacchapalakkhaṇaṃ migalakkhaṇaṃ iti vāti| aivaṃ lakkhaṇapāṭhakā lakkhaṇaṃ ādisanti|

nakkhattapāṭhakā nakkhattaṃ ādisanti| aṭṭhavīsati nakkhattāni| iminā nakkhattaina gharappavaisau kattabbau, iminā nakkhattaina makuṭaṃ bandhitabbaṃ, iminā nakkhattaina vāraiyyaṃ kāraitabbaṃ, iminā nakkhattaina bījanīhārau bījanihārau (syā॰ ka॰) kattabbau, iminā nakkhattaina saṃvāsau gharavāsau (syā॰) gantabbauti| aivaṃ nakkhattapāṭhakā nakkhattaṃ ādisanti|

āthabbaṇaṃ supinaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ, nau vidahai athaupi nakkhattanti| āthabbaṇañ‍ca supinañ‍ca lakkhaṇañ‍ca nakkhattañ‍ca nau vidahaiyya na caraiyya na samācaraiyya na samādāya vattaiyya| atha vā na gaṇhaiyya na uggaṇhaiyya na dhāraiyya na upadhāraiyya na upalakkhaiyya nappayaujaiyyāti – āthabbaṇaṃ supinaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ, nau vidahai athaupi nakkhattaṃ|

virutañ‍ca gabbhakaraṇaṃ, tikicchaṃ māmakau na saivaiyyāti| virutaṃ vuc‍cati migavāk‍kaṃ| migavāk‍kapāṭhakā migacak‍kaṃ| migacak‍kapāṭhakā (syā॰) migavāk‍kaṃ ādisanti – sakuntānaṃ vā catuppadānaṃ vā rutaṃ rudaṃ (syā॰) vassitaṃ jānantīti| aivaṃ migavāk‍kapāṭhakā migavāk‍kaṃ ādisanti| gabbhakaraṇīyā gabbhaṃ saṇṭhāpainti| dvīhi  kāraṇaihi gabbhau na saṇṭhāti – pāṇakaihi vā vātakuppaihi vā| pāṇakānaṃ vā vātakuppānaṃ vā paṭighātāya ausadhaṃ daintīti| aivaṃ gabbhakaraṇīyā gabbhaṃ saṇṭhāpainti| tikicchāti pañ‍ca tikicchā – sālākiyaṃ, sal‍lakattiyaṃ, kāyatikicchaṃ, bhūtiyaṃ, kaumārabhac‍caṃ| māmakauti buddhamāmakau dhammamāmakau saṅghamāmakau, sau vā bhagavantaṃ mamāyati bhagavā vā taṃ puggalaṃ pariggaṇhāti| vuttañhaitaṃ  bhagavatā – ‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū kuhā thaddhā lapā siṅgī un‍naḷā asamāhitā na mai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū māmakā, apagatā ca tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmā dhammavinayā, na ca tai bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti| yai ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū nik‍kuhā nil‍lapā dhīrā atthaddhā susamāhitā tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū māmakā na ca apagatā tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmā dhammavinayā, tai ca bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti’’|

‘‘kuhā  thaddhā lapā siṅgī, un‍naḷā asamāhitā|

na tai dhammai virūhanti, sammāsambuddhadaisitai||

‘‘nik‍kuhā nil‍lapā dhīrā, atthaddhā susamāhitā|

tai vai dhammai virūhanti, sammāsambuddhadaisitai’’||

virutañ‍ca gabbhakaraṇaṃ, tikicchaṃ māmakau na saivaiyyāti| virutañ‍ca gabbhakaraṇañ‍ca tikicchañ‍ca māmakau na saivaiyya na nisaivaiyya na saṃsaivaiyya nappaṭisaivaiyya na caraiyya na samācaraiyya na samādāya vattaiyya| atha  vā na gaṇhaiyya na uggaṇhaiyya na dhāraiyya na upadhāraiyya na upalakkhaiyya nappayaujaiyyāti – virutañ‍ca gabbhakaraṇaṃ tikicchaṃ māmakau na saivaiyya|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘āthabbaṇaṃ supinaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ, nau vidahai athaupi nakkhattaṃ|

virutañ‍ca gabbhakaraṇaṃ, tikicchaṃ māmakau na saivaiyyā’’ti||

163.

 nindāya  nappavaidhaiyya, na un‍namaiyya pasaṃsitau bhikkhu|

laubhaṃ saha macchariyaina, kaudhaṃ paisuṇiyañ‍ca panudaiyya|| 

nindāya nappavaidhaiyyāti| idhaikac‍cai bhikkhū nindanti jātiyā vā gauttaina vā kaulaputtiyaina vā vaṇṇapaukkharatāya vā dhanaina vā ajjhainaina vā kammāyatanaina vā sippāyatanaina vā vij‍jāṭṭhānaina vā sutaina vā paṭibhānaina vā añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunā nindanti garahanti upavadanti, ninditau garahitau upavaditau nindāya garahāya upavādaina akittiyā avaṇṇahārikāya na vaidhaiyya nappavaidhaiyya na sampavaidhaiyya na tasaiyya na uttasaiyya na paritasaiyya na bhāyaiyya na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, abhīrū assa achambhī anutrāsī apalāyī, pahīnabhayabhairavau vigatalaumahaṃsau viharaiyyāti  – nindāya nappavaidhaiyya|

na  un‍namaiyya pasaṃsitau bhikkhūti| idhaikac‍cai bhikkhū pasaṃsanti jātiyā vā gauttaina vā kaulaputtiyaina vā vaṇṇapaukkharatāya vā dhanaina vā ajjhainaina vā kammāyatanaina vā sippāyatanaina vā vij‍jāṭṭhānaina vā  sutaina vā paṭibhānaina vā añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunā pasaṃsanti thaumainti kittainti vaṇṇainti, pasaṃsitau thaumitau kittitau vaṇṇitau pasaṃsāya thaumanaina kittiyā vaṇṇahārikāya un‍natiṃ na karaiyya un‍namaṃ na karaiyya mānaṃ na karaiyya thambhaṃ na karaiyya, na taina mānaṃ janaiyya na taina thaddhau assa patthaddhau paggahitasirauti – na un‍namaiyya pasaṃsitau bhikkhu|

laubhaṃ saha macchariyaina, kaudhaṃ paisuṇiyañ‍ca panudaiyyāti| laubhauti yau laubhau lubbhanā lubbhitattaṃ sārāgau sāraj‍janā sāraj‍jitattaṃ abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| macchariyanti pañ‍ca macchariyāni – āvāsamacchariyaṃ…pai॰… gāhau vuc‍cati macchariyaṃ| kaudhauti yau cittassa āghātau paṭighātau paṭighaṃ paṭiviraudhau, kaupau pakaupau sampakaupau, dausau padausau sampadausau, cittassa byāpatti manaupadausau, kaudhau kujjhanā kujjhitattaṃ, dausau dussanā dussitattaṃ, byāpattiṃ byāpaj‍janā byāpaj‍jitattaṃ, viraudhau  paṭiviraudhau caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau anattamanatā cittassa| paisuñ‍ñanti idhaikac‍cau pisuṇavācau hauti itau sutvā amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti samaggānaṃ vā bhaittā bhin‍nānaṃ vā anuppadātā, vaggārāmau vaggaratau vagganandī vaggakaraṇiṃ  vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti – idaṃ vuc‍cati paisuñ‍ñaṃ|

api ca dvīhi kāraṇaihi paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati – piyakamyatāya vā bhaidādhippāyaina vā| kathaṃ piyakamyatāya paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati? imassa piyau bhavissāmi manāpau bhavissāmi vissāsikau bhavissāmi abbhantarikau bhavissāmi suhadayau bhavissāmīti| aivaṃ piyakamyatāya paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati| kathaṃ bhaidādhippāyaina paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati? kathaṃ imai nānā assu vinā assu vaggā assu dvaidhā assu dvaijjhā assu dvai pakkhā assu bhijjhaiyyuṃ na samāgacchaiyyuṃ dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharaiyyunti| aivaṃ bhaidādhippāyaina paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati| laubhaṃ saha macchariyaina, kaudhaṃ paisuṇiyañ‍ca panudaiyyāti| laubhañ‍ca macchariyañ‍ca kaudhañ‍ca paisuñ‍ñañ‍ca nudaiyya panudaiyya pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyyāti – laubhaṃ saha macchariyaina, kaudhaṃ paisuṇiyañ‍ca panudaiyya|

taināha  bhagavā –

‘‘nindāya  nappavaidhaiyya, na un‍namaiyya pasaṃsitau bhikkhu|

laubhaṃ saha macchariyaina, kaudhaṃ paisuṇiyañ‍ca panudaiyyā’’ti||

164.

 kayavik‍kayai na tiṭṭhaiyya, upavādaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci|

gāmai ca nābhisaj‍jaiyya, lābhakamyā janaṃ na lapayaiyya||
