kayavik‍kayai  na tiṭṭhaiyyāti| yai kayavik‍kayā vinayai paṭikkhittā na tai imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā| kathaṃ kayavik‍kayai tiṭṭhati? pañ‍can‍naṃ saddhiṃ pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā kiñ‍ci parikkhāraṃ vañ‍caniyaṃ vā karauntau udayaṃ vā patthayantau parivattaiti| aivaṃ kayavik‍kayai tiṭṭhati| kathaṃ kayavik‍kayai na tiṭṭhati? pañ‍can‍naṃ saddhiṃ pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā kiñ‍ci parikkhāraṃ na vañ‍caniyaṃ vā karauntau na udayaṃ vā patthayantau parivattaiti| aivaṃ kayavik‍kayai na tiṭṭhati| kayavik‍kayai na tiṭṭhaiyyāti| kayavik‍kayai na tiṭṭhaiyya na santiṭṭhaiyya, kayavik‍kayaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, kayavik‍kayā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – kayavik‍kayai na tiṭṭhaiyya|

upavādaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍cīti| katamai upavādakarāti kilaisā? santaikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā iddhimantau dibbacakkhukā paracittavidunau, tai dūrataupi passanti āsan‍nāpi na dissanti caitasāpi cittaṃ pajānanti, daivatāpi khau santi iddhimantiniyau dibbacakkhukā paracittaviduniyau, tā dūrataupi passanti, āsan‍nāpi na dissanti caitasāpi cittaṃ pajānanti| tai auḷārikaihi vā kilaisaihi majjhimaihi vā kilaisaihi sukhumaihi vā kilaisaihi upavadaiyyuṃ| katamai auḷārikā kilaisā? kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ – imai  vuc‍canti auḷārikā kilaisā| katamai majjhimā kilaisā? kāmavitak‍kau byāpādavitak‍kau vihiṃsāvitak‍kau – imai vuc‍canti majjhimā kilaisā| katamai sukhumā kilaisā? ñātivitak‍kau, janapadavitak‍kau, aparavitak‍kau, parānudayatā paṭisañ‍ñuttau vitak‍kau, lābhasak‍kārasilaukapaṭisañ‍ñuttau vitak‍kau, anavañ‍ñattipaṭisañ‍ñuttau vitak‍kau – imai vuc‍canti sukhumā kilaisā| tai auḷārikaihi vā kilaisaihi majjhimaihi vā kilaisaihi sukhumaihi vā kilaisaihi na upavadaiyya upavādaṃ na karaiyya upavādakarai kilaisai na karaiyya na janaiyya na sañ‍janaiyya na nibbattaiyya nābhinibbattaiyya, upavādakarai kilaisai pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya  byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, upavādakaraihi kilaisaihi āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyya| kuhiñ‍cīti kuhiñ‍ci kimhici katthaci ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā ajjhattabahiddhā vāti – upavādaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci|

gāmai ca nābhisaj‍jaiyyāti| kathaṃ gāmai saj‍jati? idha bhikkhu gāmai gihīhi saṃsaṭṭhau viharati sahanandī sahasaukī sukhitaisu sukhitau dukkhitaisu dukkhitau, uppan‍naisu kic‍cakaraṇīyaisu attanā vauyaugaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivampi gāmai saj‍jati|

atha vā bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina anupaṭṭhitāya  satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tatra tatra saj‍jati tatra tatra gaṇhāti tatra tatra bajjhati tatra tatra anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivampi gāmai saj‍jati|

kathaṃ gāmai na saj‍jati? idha bhikkhu gāmai gihīhi asaṃsaṭṭhau viharati na sahanandī na sahasaukī na sukhitaisu sukhitau na dukkhitaisu dukkhitau, uppan‍naisu kic‍cakaraṇīyaisu na attanā vauyaugaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivampi gāmai na saj‍jati|

atha vā bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati rakkhitainaiva kāyaina rakkhitāya vācāya rakkhitaina cittaina upaṭṭhitāya satiyā saṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tatra tatra na saj‍jati tatra tatra na gaṇhāti tatra tatra na bajjhati tatra tatra na anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivampi gāmai na saj‍jati| gāmai ca nābhisaj‍jaiyyāti gāmai na saj‍jaiyya na gaṇhaiyya na bajjhaiyya na palibajjhaiyya, agiddhau  assa agadhitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau vītagaidhau vigatagaidhau cattagaidhau…pai॰… brahmabhūtaina attanā viharaiyyāti – gāmai ca nābhisaj‍jaiyya|

lābhakamyā janaṃ na lapayaiyyāti| katamā lapanā? lābhasak‍kārasilaukasan‍nissitassa pāpicchassa icchāpakatassa āmisacakkhukassa  laukadhammagarukassa yā paraisaṃ ālapanā lapanā sal‍lapanā ul‍lapanā samul‍lapanā un‍nahanā samun‍nahanā uk‍kācanā samuk‍kācanā uk‍kāpanā samuk‍kāpanā (syā॰) anupiyabhāṇitā  cātukamyatā muggasūpyatā pāribhaṭayatā parapiṭṭhimaṃsikatā piṭṭhimaṃsikatā (ka॰) visuddhimaggai sīlaniddaisavaṇṇanā aulaukaitabbā, yā  tattha saṇhavācatā sakhilavācatā sithilavācatā maittavācakatā (syā॰) apharusavācatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati lapanā|

api ca dvīhi kāraṇaihi janaṃ lapati – attānaṃ vā nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau janaṃ lapati, attānaṃ vā uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau janaṃ lapati| kathaṃ attānaṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau janaṃ lapati? ‘‘tumhai mai bahūpakārā, ahaṃ tumhai nissāya labhāmi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, yaipi mai añ‍ñai dātuṃ vā kātuṃ vā mañ‍ñanti, tumhai nissāya tumhai sampassantā yampi mai purāṇaṃ mātāpaittikaṃ nāmadhaiyyaṃ tampi mai antarahitaṃ| tumhaihi ahaṃ ñāyāmi asukassa kulūpakau asukāya kulūpakau’’ti| aivaṃ attānaṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau janaṃ lapati|

kathaṃ attānaṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau janaṃ lapati? ‘‘ahaṃ tumhākaṃ bahūpakārau, tumhai maṃ āgamma buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā, musāvādā paṭiviratā, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā| ahaṃ tumhākaṃ uddaisaṃ daimi, paripucchaṃ daimi, upausathaṃ ācikkhāmi, navakammaṃ adhiṭṭhāmi| atha pana tumhai maṃ ujjhitvā añ‍ñai sak‍karautha garuṃ karautha mānaitha pūjaithā’’ti| aivaṃ attānaṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau  janaṃ lapati|

lābhakamyā  janaṃ na lapayaiyyāti| lābhahaitu lābhapac‍cayā lābhakāraṇā lābhābhinibbattiyā lābhaṃ paripācaintau janaṃ na lapayaiyya, lapanaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, lapanā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – lābhakamyā janaṃ na lapayaiyya|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘kayavik‍kayai na tiṭṭhaiyya, upavādaṃ bhikkhu na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci|

gāmai ca nābhisaj‍jaiyya, lābhakamyā janaṃ na lapayaiyyā’’ti||

165.

 na ca katthikau katthitā (syā॰ ka॰) siyā bhikkhu, na ca vācaṃ payuttaṃ bhāsaiyya|

pāgabbhiyaṃ na sikkhaiyya, kathaṃ viggāhikaṃ na kathayaiyya||

na  ca katthikau siyā bhikkhūti| idhaikac‍cau katthī hauti vikatthī| sau katthati vikatthati ahamasmi sīlasampan‍nauti vā vatasampan‍nauti vā sīlabbatasampan‍nauti vā jātiyā vā gauttaina vā kaulaputtiyaina vā vaṇṇapaukkharatāya vā dhanaina vā ajjhainaina vā kammāyatanaina vā sippāyatanaina vā vij‍jāṭṭhānaina vā sutaina vā paṭibhānaina vā añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunā| uc‍cā kulā pabbajitauti  vā mahābhaugakulā pabbajitauti vā uḷārabhaugakulā pabbajitauti vā suttantikauti vā vinayadharauti vā dhammakathikauti vā ārañ‍ñikauti vā…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhīti vā katthati vikatthati| aivaṃ na katthaiyya na vikatthaiyya, katthaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, katthanā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – na ca katthikau siyā bhikkhu|

na  ca vācaṃ payuttaṃ bhāsaiyyāti| katamā payuttavācā? idhaikac‍cau  cīvarapayuttaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati, piṇḍapātapayuttaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati, saināsanapayuttaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati, gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārapayuttaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ayampi vuc‍cati payuttavācā|

atha vā cīvarahaitu piṇḍapātahaitu saināsanahaitu gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu sac‍campi bhaṇati musāpi bhaṇati, pisuṇampi bhaṇati apisuṇampi bhaṇati, pharusampi bhaṇati apharusampi bhaṇati, samphappalāpampi bhaṇati asamphappalāpampi bhaṇati, mantāpi vācaṃ bhāsati – ayampi vuc‍cati payuttavācā| atha vā pasan‍nacittau paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘‘ahau vata mai dhammaṃ suṇaiyyuṃ, sutvāva dhammaṃ pasīdaiyyuṃ, pasan‍nā ca mai pasan‍nākāraṃ karaiyyu’’nti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati payuttavācā| na ca vācaṃ payuttaṃ bhāsaiyyāti| antamasau dhammadaisanaṃ vācaṃ upādāya payuttavācaṃ na bhāsaiyya  na kathaiyya na bhaṇaiyya na dīpaiyya na vauharaiyya, payuttaṃ vācaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, payuttavācāya āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – na ca vācaṃ payuttaṃ bhāsaiyya|

pāgabbhiyaṃ na sikkhaiyyāti| pāgabbhiyanti tīṇi pāgabbhiyāni – kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ, vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ, caitasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ| katamaṃ kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ? idhaikac‍cau saṅghagataupi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti, gaṇagataupi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti, bhaujanasālāyapi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti, jantāgharaipi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti, udakatitthaipi  kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti, antaragharaṃ pavisantaupi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti, antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhaupi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ saṅghagatau kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau saṅghagatau acittīkārakatau thairai bhikkhū ghaṭṭayantaupi tiṭṭhati, ghaṭṭayantaupi nisīdati, purataupi tiṭṭhati, purataupi nisīdati, uc‍caipi āsanai nisīdati, sasīsaṃ pārupitvā nisīdati, ṭhitakaupi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhaipakaupi bhaṇati| aivaṃ saṅghagatau kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ  gaṇagatau kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau gaṇagatau acittīkārakatau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupāhanānaṃ caṅkamantānaṃ saupāhanau caṅkamati, nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantānaṃ uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamati, chamāya caṅkamantānaṃ caṅkamai caṅkamati, ghaṭṭayantaupi tiṭṭhati, ghaṭṭayantaupi nisīdati, purataupi tiṭṭhati, purataupi nisīdati, uc‍caipi āsanai nisīdati, sasīsaṃ pārupitvāpi nisīdati, ṭhitaupi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhaipakaupi bhaṇati| aivaṃ gaṇagatau kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ bhaujanasālāya kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau bhaujanasālāya acittīkārakatau thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍ja nisīdati, navaipi bhikkhū āsanaina paṭibāhati, ghaṭṭayantaupi tiṭṭhati, ghaṭṭayantaupi nisīdati, purataupi tiṭṭhati, purataupi nisīdati, uc‍caipi āsanai nisīdati, sasīsaṃ pārupitvā nisīdati, ṭhitakaupi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhaipakaupi bhaṇati| aivaṃ bhaujanasālāya kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ jantāgharai kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau jantāgharai acittīkārakatau thairai bhikkhū ghaṭṭayantaupi tiṭṭhati, ghaṭṭayantaupi nisīdati, purataupi tiṭṭhati, purataupi nisīdati, uc‍caipi āsanai nisīdati, anāpucchāpi kaṭṭhaṃ pakkhipati, anāpucchāpi dvāraṃ pidahati| aivaṃ jantāgharai kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ udakatitthai kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau udakatitthai acittīkārakatau thairai bhikkhū ghaṭṭayantaupi autarati, purataupi autarati, ghaṭṭayantaupi nhāyati, purataupi nhāyati, uparitaupi nhāyati, ghaṭṭayantaupi uttarati, purataupi uttarati| aivaṃ udakatitthai kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ  antaragharaṃ pavisantau kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau antaragharaṃ pavisantau acittīkārakatau thairai bhikkhū ghaṭṭayantaupi gacchati, purataupi gacchati, vauk‍kammapi thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ puratau gacchati| aivaṃ antaragharaṃ pavisantau kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau ‘‘na pavisa, bhantai’’ti vuc‍camānau pavisati, ‘‘na tiṭṭha, bhantai’’ti vuc‍camānau  tiṭṭhati, ‘‘na nisīda, bhantai’’ti vuc‍camānau nisīdati, anaukāsampi pavisati, anaukāsaipi tiṭṭhati, anaukāsaipi nisīdati, yānipi tāni haunti kulānaṃ auvarakāni gūḷhāni ca paṭicchan‍nāni ca, yattha kulitthiyau kuladhītarau kulasuṇhāyau kulakumāriyau nisīdanti tatthapi sahasā pavisati, kumārakassa sīsampi parāmasati| aivaṃ antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti – idaṃ kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ|

katamaṃ vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ? idhaikac‍cau saṅghagataupi vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti, gaṇagataupi vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti, antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhaupi vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti| kathaṃ saṅghagatau vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau saṅghagatau acittīkārakatau thairai bhikkhū anāpucchaṃ vā anajjhiṭṭhau vā ārāmagatānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇati, pañhaṃ visaj‍jaiti, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati, ṭhitakaupi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhaipakaupi bhaṇati| aivaṃ saṅghagatau vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ gaṇagatau vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau gaṇagatau acittīkārakatau thairai bhikkhū anāpucchaṃ vā anajjhiṭṭhau vā ārāmagatānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇati, pañhaṃ visaj‍jaiti, ṭhitakaupi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhaipakaupi bhaṇati; ārāmagatānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇati, pañhaṃ visaj‍jaiti, ṭhitakaupi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhaipakaupi bhaṇati| aivaṃ gaṇagatau vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti|

kathaṃ antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti? idhaikac‍cau antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau itthiṃ vā kumāriṃ vā aivamāha – ‘‘itthan‍nāmai itthaṃgauttai kiṃ atthi? yāgu atthi, bhattaṃ atthi, khādanīyaṃ atthi? kiṃ pivissāma, kiṃ bhuñ‍jissāma, kiṃ khādissāma, kiṃ vā atthi, kiṃ  vā mai dassathā’’ti vippalapati| aivaṃ antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dassaiti – idaṃ vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ|

katamaṃ caitasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ? idhaikac‍cau na uc‍cā kulā pabbajitau samānau uc‍cā kulā pabbajitaina saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ karauti cittaina, na mahābhaugakulā pabbajitau samānau mahābhaugakulā pabbajitaina saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ karauti cittaina, na uḷārabhaugakulā pabbajitau  samānau uḷārabhaugakulā pabbajitaina saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ karauti cittaina, na suttantikau  samānau suttantikaina saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ karauti cittaina, na vinayadharau samānau vinayadharaina… na dhammakathikau samānau dhammakathikaina… na ārañ‍ñikau samānau ārañ‍ñakaina… na piṇḍapātikau samānau piṇḍapātikaina… na paṃsukūlikau samānau paṃsukūlikaina… na taicīvarikau samānau taicīvarakaina… na sapadānacārikau samānau sapadānacārikaina… na khalupacchābhattikau samānau khalupacchābhattikaina… na naisaj‍jikau samānau naisañ‍ñikaina… na yathāsanthatikau samānau yathāsanthatikaina saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ karauti cittaina, na paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī samānau paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhinā saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ karauti cittaina, na dutiyassa jhānassa lābhī samānau dutiyassa… na tatiyassa jhānassa lābhī samānau tatiyassa… na catutthassa jhānassa lābhī samānau lābhī samānau catutthassa… na ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī samānau ākāsanañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā… na viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī samānau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatana samāpattiyā… na ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī samānau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatana samāpattiyā… na naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī samānau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhinā saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ karauti cittaina – idaṃ caitasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ| na sikkhaiyyāti pāgabbhiyaṃ na sikkhaiyya na caraiyya na ācaraiyya na samācaraiyya na samādāya vattaiyya, pāgabbhiyaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, pāgabbhiyā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – pāgabbhiyaṃ na sikkhaiyya|

kathaṃ  viggāhikaṃ na kathayaiyyāti| katamā viggāhikā kathā? idhaikac‍cau aivarūpiṃ kathaṃ kattā hauti – ‘‘na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi…pai॰… nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausī’’ti| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘viggāhikāya khau, mauggal‍lāna, kathāya sati kathābāhul‍laṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ, kathābāhul‍lai sati uddhac‍caṃ, uddhatassa asaṃvarau, asaṃvutassa ārā cittaṃ  samādhimhā’’ti| kathaṃ viggāhikaṃ na kathayaiyyāti| viggāhikaṃ kathaṃ na kathaiyya na bhaṇaiyya na dīpaiyya na vauharaiyya, viggāhikaṃ kathaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, viggāhikakathāya āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – kathaṃ viggāhikaṃ na kathayaiyya|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘na  ca katthikau siyā bhikkhu, na ca vācaṃ payuttaṃ bhāsaiyya|

pāgabbhiyaṃ na sikkhaiyya, kathaṃ viggāhikaṃ na kathayaiyyā’’ti||

166.

 mausavaj‍jai na niyyaitha, sampajānau saṭhāni na kayirā|

atha jīvitaina pañ‍ñāya, sīlabbataina nāñ‍ñamatimañ‍ñai|| 

mausavaj‍jai na niyyaithāti| mausavaj‍jaṃ vuc‍cati musāvādau| idhaikac‍cau  sabhaggatau sabhāgaggatau (sī॰ ka॰) vā parisaggatau parisagatau (sī॰ ka॰) vā…pai॰… āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitu vā sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti – idaṃ vuc‍cati mausavaj‍jaṃ| api ca tīhākāraihi musāvādau hauti – pubbaivassa hauti ‘‘musā bhaṇissa’’nti, bhaṇantassa hauti ‘‘musā bhaṇāmī’’ti, bhaṇitassa hauti ‘‘musā mayā bhaṇita’’nti, imaihi tīhākāraihi musāvādau hauti| api ca catūhākāraihi… pañ‍cahākāraihi… chahākāraihi… sattahākāraihi… aṭṭhahākāraihi… musāvādau hauti – pubbaivassa hauti ‘‘musā bhaṇissa’’nti, bhaṇantassa hauti ‘‘musā bhaṇāmī’’ti, bhaṇitassa hauti ‘‘musā mayā  bhaṇita’’nti vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ vinidhāya khantiṃ vinidhāya ruciṃ vinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ vinidhāya bhāvaṃ – imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi musāvādau hauti| mausavaj‍jai na niyyaithāti| mausavaj‍jai na yāyaiyya na niyyāyaiyya na vahaiyya na vuyhaiyya (sī॰ ka॰), natthi syā॰ pautthakai na saṃharaiyya, mausavaj‍jaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, mausavaj‍jā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – mausavaj‍jai na niyyaitha|

sampajānau saṭhāni na kayirāti| katamaṃ sāṭhaiyyaṃ? idhaikac‍cau saṭhau hauti parisaṭhau, yaṃ tattha saṭhaṃ saṭhatā sāṭhaiyyaṃ kak‍karatā kak‍kariyaṃ parikkhattatā pārikkhattiyaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati sāṭhaiyyaṃ| sampajānau saṭhāni na kayirāti| sampajānau hutvā sāṭhaiyyaṃ na karaiyya na janaiyya na sañ‍janaiyya na nibbattaiyya nābhinibbattaiyya, sāṭhaiyyaṃ  pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya  anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, sāṭhaiyyā āratau assa  viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – sampajānau saṭhāni na kayirā|

atha jīvitaina pañ‍ñāya, sīlabbataina nāñ‍ñamatimañ‍ñaiti| athāti padasandhi…pai॰… padānupubbatāpaitaṃ – athāti| idhaikac‍cau lūkhajīvitaṃ jīvantau paraṃ paṇītajīvitaṃ jīvantaṃ atimañ‍ñati – ‘‘kiṃ panāyaṃ bahulājīvau sabbaṃ saṃbhakkhaiti, saiyyathidaṃ – mūlabījaṃ khandhabījaṃ phaḷubījaṃ aggabījaṃ bījabījamaiva pañ‍camaṃ asanivicak‍kadantakūṭasamaṇappadhānainā’’ti asanīva cak‍kaṃ dantakūṭaṃ samaṇappadhānaināti (sī॰)| sau tāya lūkhajīvitāya paraṃ paṇītajīvitaṃ jīvantaṃ atimañ‍ñati|

idhaikac‍cau paṇītajīvitaṃ jīvantau paraṃ lūkhajīvitaṃ jīvantaṃ atimañ‍ñati – ‘‘kiṃ panāyaṃ appapuñ‍ñau appaisakkhau na lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti| sau tāya paṇītajīvitāya  paraṃ lūkhajīvitaṃ jīvantaṃ atimañ‍ñati| idhaikac‍cau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti| sau puṭṭhau pañhaṃ visaj‍jaiti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ahamasmi pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau, imai panañ‍ñai na pañ‍ñāsampan‍nā’’ti| sau tāya pañ‍ñāsampadāya paraṃ atimañ‍ñati| idhaikac‍cau sīlasampan‍nau hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ahamasmi sīlasampan‍nau, imai panañ‍ñai  bhikkhū dussīlā pāpadhammā’’ti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya paraṃ atimañ‍ñati| idhaikac‍cau vatasampan‍nau hauti ārañ‍ñikau vā piṇḍapātikau vā paṃsukūlikau vā taicīvarikau vā sapadānacārikau vā khalupacchābhattikau vā naisaj‍jikau vā yathāsanthatikau vā| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ahamasmi vata sampan‍nau, imai panañ‍ñai na vatasampan‍nā’’ti| sau tāya vatasampadāya paraṃ atimañ‍ñati| atha jīvitaina pañ‍ñāya, sīlabbataina nāñ‍ñamatimañ‍ñaiti| lūkhajīvitāya vā paṇītajīvitāya vā pañ‍ñāsampadāya vā sīlasampadāya vā vatasampadāya vā paraṃ nātimañ‍ñaiyya, nāvajānaiyya, na taina mānaṃ janaiyya, na taina thaddhau assa, patthaddhau paggahitasirauti – atha jīvitaina pañ‍ñāya sīlabbataina nāñ‍ñamatimañ‍ñai|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘mausavaj‍jai na niyyaitha, sampajānau saṭhāni na kayirā|

atha jīvitaina pañ‍ñāya, sīlabbataina nāñ‍ñamatimañ‍ñai’’ti||

167.

 sutvā  rusitau dūsitau (sī॰ syā॰) bahuṃ vācaṃ, samaṇānaṃ vā puthujanānaṃ puthuvacanānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) |

pharusaina nai na paṭivaj‍jā, na hi santau paṭisainiṃ karaunti paṭisaini karauti (syā॰) ||

sutvā  rusitau bahuṃ vācaṃ, samaṇānaṃ puthujanānanti| rusitauti dūsitau khuṃsitau ghaṭṭitau vambhitau garahitau upavaditau| samaṇānanti yai kaici itau bahiddhā paribbajūpagatā paribbajasamāpan‍nā| puthujanānanti khattiyā ca brāhmaṇā ca vaissā ca suddā ca gahaṭṭhā ca pabbajitā ca daivā ca manussā ca, tai bahukāhi vācāhi aniṭṭhāhi akantāhi amanāpāhi ak‍kausaiyyuṃ paribhāsaiyyuṃ rausaiyyuṃ virausaiyyuṃ hiṃsaiyyuṃ vihiṃsaiyyuṃ haiṭhaiyyuṃ vihaiṭhaiyyuṃ ghātaiyyuṃ upaghātaiyyuṃ upaghātaṃ karaiyyuṃ taisaṃ bahuṃ vācaṃ aniṭṭhaṃ akantaṃ amanāpaṃ sutvā suṇitvā uggahitvā upadhārayitvā upalakkhayitvāti – sutvā rusitau bahuṃ vācaṃ, samaṇānaṃ vā puthujanānaṃ|

pharusaina nai na paṭivaj‍jāti| pharusaināti pharusaina kakkhaḷaina na paṭivaj‍jā nappaṭibhaṇaiyya, ak‍kausantaṃ na pac‍cak‍kausaiyya, rausantaṃ nappaṭirausaiyya, bhaṇḍanaṃ nappaṭibhaṇḍaiyya na kalahaṃ karaiyya na bhaṇḍanaṃ karaiyya na viggahaṃ karaiyya na vivādaṃ karaiyya na maidhagaṃ karaiyya, kalahabhaṇḍanaviggahavivādamaidhagaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, kalahabhaṇḍanaviggahavivādamaidhagā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – pharusaina nai na paṭivaj‍jā|

na hi santau paṭisainiṃ karauntīti| santauti rāgassa santattā santau, dausassa… mauhassa… kaudhassa… upanāhassa…pai॰… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārānaṃ  santattā samitattā vūpasamitattā vijjhātattā nibbutattā vigatattā paṭipassaddhattā santau upasantau vūpasantau nibbutau paṭipassaddhauti – santau| na hi santau paṭisainiṃ karauntīti| santau paṭisainiṃ paṭimal‍laṃ paṭikaṇṭakaṃ paṭibandhanaṃ (sī॰) paṭipakkhaṃ na karaunti na janainti  na sañ‍janainti  na nibbattainti nābhinibbattaintīti – na hi santau paṭisainiṃ karaunti|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘sutvā rusitau bahuṃ vācaṃ, samaṇānaṃ vā puthujanānaṃ|

pharusaina nai na paṭivaj‍jā, na hi santau paṭisainiṃ karauntī’’ti||

168.

 aitañ‍ca  dhammamañ‍ñāya, vicinaṃ bhikkhu sadā satau sikkhai|

santīti nibbutiṃ ñatvā, sāsanai gautamassa nappamaj‍jaiyya||

aitañ‍ca dhammamañ‍ñāyāti| aitanti ācikkhitaṃ daisitaṃ pañ‍ñapitaṃ paṭṭhapitaṃ vivaṭaṃ vibhattaṃ uttānīkataṃ pakāsitaṃ dhammaṃ añ‍ñāya jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti| aivampi aitañ‍ca dhammamañ‍ñāya| atha vā samañ‍ca visamañ‍ca pathañ‍ca vipathañ‍ca sāvaj‍jañ‍ca anavaj‍jañ‍ca hīnañ‍ca paṇītañ‍ca kaṇhañ‍ca suk‍kañ‍ca viñ‍ñūgarahitañ‍ca viñ‍ñūpasatthañ‍ca dhammaṃ añ‍ñāya jānitvā  tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti| aivampi aitañ‍ca dhammamañ‍ñāya| atha vā sammāpaṭipadaṃ anulaumapaṭipadaṃ apac‍canīkapaṭipadaṃ aviruddhapaṭipadaṃ anvatthapaṭipadaṃ dhammānudhammapaṭipadaṃ sīlaisu paripūrakāritaṃ indriyaisu guttadvārataṃ bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutaṃ jāgariyānuyaugaṃ satisampajañ‍ñaṃ, cattārau satipaṭṭhānai cattārau sammappadhānai cattārau iddhipādai pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgai ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, nibbānañ‍ca nibbānagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ dhammaṃ añ‍ñāya jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti| aivampi tañ‍ca dhammamañ‍ñāya|

vicinaṃ bhikkhu sadā satau sikkhaiti| vicinanti vicinantau pavicinantau tulayantau tīrayantau vibhāvayantau vibhūtaṃ karauntau| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti vicinantau  pavicinantau tulayantau tīrayantau vibhāvayantau vibhūtaṃ karauntauti – vicinaṃ bhikkhu| sadāti sadā sabbadā sabbakālaṃ…pai॰… pacchimai vayaukhandhai| satauti catūhi kāraṇaihi satau – kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvaintau satau…pai॰… sau vuc‍cati satau| sikkhaiti  tissau sikkhāyau – adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā…pai॰… ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| imā tissau sikkhāyau āvaj‍jantau sikkhaiyya…pai॰… sikkhaiyya ācaraiyya samācaraiyya samādāya vattaiyyāti – vicinaṃ bhikkhu sadā satau sikkhai|

santīti  nibbutiṃ ñatvāti| rāgassa nibbutiṃ santīti ñatvā, dausassa… mauhassa…pai॰… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārānaṃ nibbutiṃ santīti ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti – santīti nibbutiṃ ñatvā|

sāsanai gautamassa nappamaj‍jaiyyāti| gautamassa sāsanai buddhasāsanai jinasāsanai tathāgatasāsanai  daivasāsanai arahantasāsanai| nappamaj‍jaiyyāti sak‍kac‍cakārī assa sātac‍cakārī aṭṭhitakārī anaulīnavuttikau anikkhittacchandau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| ‘‘kadāhaṃ aparipūraṃ vā sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūraiyyaṃ…pai॰… aparipūraṃ vā samādhikkhandhaṃ… pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ… vimuttikkhandhaṃ… vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ? kadāhaṃ apariñ‍ñātaṃ vā dukkhaṃ parijānaiyyaṃ, appahīnai vā kilaisai pajahaiyyaṃ, abhāvitaṃ vā maggaṃ bhāvaiyyaṃ, asacchikataṃ vā niraudhaṃ sacchikaraiyya’’nti? yau tattha chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca thāmau ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca ātappaṃ padhānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ anuyaugau appamādau kusalaisu dhammaisūti – sāsanai gautamassa nappamaj‍jaiyya|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘aitañ‍ca dhammamañ‍ñāya, vicinaṃ bhikkhu sadā satau sikkhai|

santīti nibbutiṃ ñatvā, sāsanai gautamassa nappamaj‍jaiyyā’’ti||

169.

 abhibhū  hi sau anabhibhūtau, sakkhidhammamanītihamaddasi|

tasmā hi tassa bhagavatau sāsanai, appamattau sadā namassamanusikkhai||[iti bhagavā]

abhibhū  hi sau anabhibhūtauti| abhibhūti rūpābhibhū abhibhūtarūpā (syā॰) aivamañ‍ñaisu pañ‍capadaisupi saddābhibhū gandhābhibhū rasābhibhū phauṭṭhabbābhibhū dhammābhibhū, anabhibhūtau kaihici kilaisaihi, abhibhausi nai pāpakai abhibhū hi nai hīnai pāpakai (sī॰ ka॰), abhibhū hi pāpakai (syā॰) akusalai dhammai saṃkilaisikai paunaubhavikai sadarai dukkhavipākai āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyaiti – abhibhū hi sau anabhibhūtau|

sakkhidhammamanītihamaddasīti| sakkhidhammanti na itihitihaṃ na itikiriyāya na paramparāya na piṭakasampadāya na tak‍kahaitu na nayahaitu na ākāraparivitak‍kaina na diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā sāmaṃ sayamabhiñ‍ñātaṃ attapac‍cakkhadhammaṃ addasi addakkhi apassi paṭivijjhīti – sakkhidhammamanītihamaddasi|

tasmā hi tassa bhagavatau sāsanaiti| tasmāti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhaitu tappac‍cayā taṃnidānā| tassa bhagavatau sāsanaiti| tassa bhagavatau sāsanai gautamasāsanai buddhasāsanai jinasāsanai tathāgatasāsanai daivasāsanai arahantasāsanaiti – tasmā tassa bhagavatau sāsanai|

appamattau  sadā namassamanusikkhai (iti bhagavā)ti| appamattauti sak‍kac‍cakārī…pai॰… appamādau kusalaisu dhammaisu| sadāti sadā sabbakālaṃ…pai॰… pacchimai vayaukhandhai| namassanti kāyaina vā namassamānau vācāya vā namassamānau cittaina vā namassamānau anvatthapaṭipattiyā vā namassamānau dhammānudhammapaṭipattiyā  vā namassamānau sak‍kurumānau garukurumānau sak‍kāramānau garukāramānau (syā॰) mānayamānau pūjayamānau apacayamānau| anusikkhaiti tissau sikkhāyau – adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā…pai॰… ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| imā tissau sikkhāyau āvaj‍jantau sikkhaiyya…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau sikkhaiyya caraiyya ācaraiyya samācaraiyya samādāya vattaiyya| bhagavāti gāravādhivacanaṃ…pai॰… sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ bhagavāti – appamattau sadā namassamanusikkhai| (iti bhagavā)|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘abhibhū  hi sau anabhibhūtau, sakkhidhammamanītihamaddasi|

tasmā hi tassa bhagavatau sāsanai, appamattau sadā namassamanusikkhai’’|| [iti bhagavāti]

tuvaṭṭakasuttaniddaisau tuvaṭakasuttaniddaisau (sī॰ syā॰) suttanipātaipi cuddasamau|



15. attadaṇḍasuttaniddaisau

atha  attadaṇḍasuttaniddaisaṃ vakkhati –

170.

 attadaṇḍā  bhayaṃ jātaṃ, janaṃ passatha maidhagaṃ|

saṃvaigaṃ kittayissāmi, yathā saṃvijitaṃ mayā||

attadaṇḍā bhayaṃ jātanti| daṇḍāti tayau daṇḍā – kāyadaṇḍau, vacīdaṇḍau, manaudaṇḍau| tividhaṃ kāyaduc‍caritaṃ kāyadaṇḍau, catubbidhaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ vacīdaṇḍau, tividhaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ manaudaṇḍau| bhayanti dvai bhayāni – diṭṭhadhammikañ‍ca bhayaṃ samparāyikañ‍ca bhayaṃ| katamaṃ diṭṭhadhammikaṃ bhayaṃ? idhaikac‍cau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati, pāṇampi hanati, adin‍nampi ādiyati, sandhimpi chindati, nil‍laupampi harati, aikāgārikampi karauti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati, paradārampi gacchati, musāpi bhaṇati| tamainaṃ gahaitvā rañ‍ñau dassainti – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, caurau āgucārī| imassa yaṃ icchasi taṃ daṇḍaṃ paṇaihī’’ti| tamainaṃ rājā paribhāsati| sau paribhāsapac‍cayā bhayampi uppādaiti, dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ dukkhadaumanassaṃ (syā॰) paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau tassa? attadaṇḍatau jātaṃ sañ‍jātaṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ|

aittakainapi rājā na tussati| tamainaṃ rājā bandhāpaiti andubandhanaina  vā raj‍jubandhanaina vā saṅkhalikabandhanaina vā vaittabandhanaina vā latābandhanaina vā pakkhaipabandhanaina vā paikkhabandhanaina vā (syā॰) parikkhaipabandhanaina vā  gāmabandhanaina vā nigamabandhanaina vā nagarabandhanaina vā raṭṭhabandhanaina vā janapadabandhanaina vā antamasau savacanīyampi karauti – ‘‘na tai labbhā itau pak‍kamitu’’nti| sau bandhanapac‍cayāpi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ  paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau tassa? attadaṇḍatau jātaṃ sañ‍jātaṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ|

aittakainapi rājā na tussati| rājā tassa dhanaṃ āharāpaiti – sataṃ vā sahassaṃ vā satasahassaṃ  vā| sau dhanajānipac‍cayāpi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau tassa? attadaṇḍatau jātaṃ sañ‍jātaṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ|

aittakainapi rājā na tussati| tamainaṃ rājā vividhā kammakāraṇā kārāpaiti – kasāhipi tāḷaiti, vaittaihipi tāḷaiti, aḍḍhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷaiti, hatthampi chindati, pādampi chindati, hatthapādampi chindati, kaṇṇampi chindati, nāsampi chindati, kaṇṇanāsampi chindati, bilaṅgathālikampi karauti, saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karauti, rāhumukhampi karauti, jautimālikampi karauti, hatthapaj‍jautikampi karauti, airakapattikampi karauti, cīrakavāsikampi karauti, aiṇaiyyakampi karauti, baḷisamaṃsikampi karauti, kahāpaṇikampi karauti, khārāpatacchikampi karauti, palighaparivattakampi karauti, palālapīṭhakampi karauti, tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍cati, sunakhaihipi khādāpaiti, jīvantampi sūlai uttāsaiti, asināpi sīsaṃ chindati| sau kammakāraṇapac‍cayāpi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ  daumanassaṃ kutau tassa? attadaṇḍatau jātaṃ sañ‍jātaṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ| rājā imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ daṇḍānaṃ issarau|

sau sakaina kammaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ  maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ nirayapālā pañ‍cavidhabandhanaṃ nāma kammakāraṇaṃ kārainti – tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ hatthai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyai hatthai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ pādai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyai pādai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ majjhai urasmiṃ gamainti| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā tippā (syā॰) kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti; na ca tāva kālaṃkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau tassa? attadaṇḍatau jātaṃ sañ‍jātaṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ|

tamainaṃ  nirayapālā saṃvaisaitvā saṃvaisitvā (syā॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.267 kuṭhārīhi kudhārīhi (syā॰ ka॰) tacchainti| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti; na ca tāva kālaṃkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| tamainaṃ nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ uddhapādaṃ (sī॰) adhausiraṃ gahaitvā vāsīhi tacchainti| tamainaṃ nirayapālā rathai yaujaitvā ādittāya pathaviyā sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya sāraintipi pac‍cāsāraintipi hāraintipi pac‍cāhāraintipi (sī॰ ka॰) …pai॰… tamainaṃ nirayapālā mahantaṃ aṅgārapabbataṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ āraupaintipi auraupaintipi…pai॰… tamainaṃ nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ  adhausiraṃ gahaitvā tattāya lauhakumbhiyā pakkhipanti ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya| sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍cati| sau tattha  phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍camānau sakimpi uddhaṃ gacchati, sakimpi adhau gacchati, sakimpi tiriyaṃ gacchati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti; na ca tāva kālaṃkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau tassa? attadaṇḍatau jātaṃ sañ‍jātaṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ| tamainaṃ nirayapālā mahānirayai pakkhipanti| sau khau pana mahānirayau –

‘‘catuk‍kaṇṇau  catudvārau, vibhattau bhāgasau mitau|

ayaupākārapariyantau … pariyattau (syā॰ ka॰), ayasā paṭikuj‍jitau||

‘‘tassa ayaumayā bhūmi, jalitā taijasā yutā|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā||

‘‘kadariyātapanā ghaurā, ac‍cimantau durāsadā|

laumahaṃsanarūpā ca, bhismā paṭibhayā dukhā||

‘‘puratthimāya bhittiyā, ac‍cikkhandhau samuṭṭhitau|

dahantau pāpakammantai, pacchimāya paṭihañ‍ñati||

‘‘pacchimāya ca bhittiyā, ac‍cikkhandhau samuṭṭhitau|

dahantau pāpakammantai, puratthimāya paṭihañ‍ñati||

‘‘uttarāya  ca bhittiyā, ac‍cikkhandhau samuṭṭhitau|

dahantau pāpakammantai, dakkhiṇāya paṭihañ‍ñati||

‘‘dakkhiṇāya  ca bhittiyā, ac‍cikkhandhau samuṭṭhitau|

dahantau pāpakammantai, uttarāya paṭihañ‍ñati||

‘‘haiṭṭhatau ca samuṭṭhāya, ac‍cikkhandhau bhayānakau|

dahantau pāpakammantai, chadanasmiṃ paṭihañ‍ñati||

‘‘chadanamhā  samuṭṭhāya, ac‍cikkhandhau bhayānakau|

dahantau pāpakammantai, bhūmiyaṃ paṭihañ‍ñati||

‘‘ayaukapālamādittaṃ, santattaṃ jalitaṃ yathā|

aivaṃ avīcinirayau, haiṭṭhā upari passatau||

‘‘tattha sattā mahāluddā, mahākibbisakārinau|

ac‍cantapāpakammantā, pac‍canti na ca miyyarai mīyarai (sī॰)||

‘‘jātavaidasamau kāyau, taisaṃ nirayavāsinaṃ|

passa kammānaṃ daḷhattaṃ, na bhasmā hauti napī masi||

‘‘puratthimainapi dhāvanti, tatau dhāvanti pacchimaṃ|

uttarainapi dhāvanti, tatau dhāvanti dakkhiṇaṃ||

‘‘yaṃ yaṃ disaṃ padhāvanti disampi dhāvanti (syā॰), taṃ taṃ dvāraṃ pidhīyati pithīyati (sī॰ syā॰)|

abhinikkhamitāsā tai, sattā maukkhagavaisinau||

‘‘na tai tatau nikkhamituṃ, labhanti kammapac‍cayā|

taisañ‍ca pāpakammantaṃ, avipak‍kaṃ kataṃ bahu’’nti||

aitaṃ  bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau tassa? attadaṇḍatau jātaṃ sañ‍jātaṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ| yāni  ca nairayikāni dukkhāni yāni ca tiracchānayaunikāni dukkhāni yāni ca paittivisayikāni dukkhāni yāni ca mānusikāni dukkhāni; tāni kutau jātāni kutau sañ‍jātāni kutau nibbattāni kutau abhinibbattāni kutau pātubhūtāni? attadaṇḍatau jātāni sañ‍jātāni nibbattāni abhinibbattāni pātubhūtānīti – attadaṇḍā bhayaṃ jātaṃ|

janaṃ passatha maidhaganti| jananti khattiyā ca brāhmaṇā ca vaissā ca suddā ca gahaṭṭhā ca pabbajitā ca daivā ca manussā ca maidhagaṃ janaṃ kalahaṃ janaṃ viruddhaṃ janaṃ paṭiviruddhaṃ janaṃ  āhataṃ janaṃ pac‍cāhataṃ janaṃ āghātitaṃ janaṃ pac‍cāghātitaṃ  janaṃ passatha dakkhatha aulaukaitha nijjhāyaitha upaparikkhathāti – janaṃ passatha maidhagaṃ|

saṃvaigaṃ kittayissāmīti| saṃvaigaṃ ubbaigaṃ utrāsaṃ bhayaṃ pīḷanaṃ ghaṭṭanaṃ upaddavaṃ upasaggaṃ| kittayissāmīti pakittayissāmi ācikkhissāmi daisaissāmi pañ‍ñapaissāmi paṭṭhapaissāmi vivarissāmi vibhajissāmi uttānīkarissāmi pakāsissāmīti – saṃvaigaṃ kittayissāmi|

yathā saṃvijitaṃ mayāti| yathā mayā attanāyaiva attānaṃ saṃvaijitau ubbaijitau saṃvaigamāpāditauti – yathā saṃvijitaṃ mayā|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘attadaṇḍā bhayaṃ jātaṃ, janaṃ passatha maidhagaṃ|

saṃvaigaṃ kittayissāmi, yathā saṃvijitaṃ mayā’’ti||

171.

 phandamānaṃ  pajaṃ disvā, macchai appaudakai yathā|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi byāruddhai, disvā maṃ bhayamāvisi||

phandamānaṃ pajaṃ disvāti| pajāti sattādhivacanaṃ| pajaṃ taṇhāphandanāya phandamānaṃ diṭṭhiphandanāya phandamānaṃ  kilaisaphandanāya phandamānaṃ duc‍caritaphandanāya phandamānaṃ payaugaphandanāya phandamānaṃ vipākaphandanāya phandamānaṃ rattaṃ rāgaina phandamānaṃ duṭṭhaṃ dausaina phandamānaṃ mūḷhaṃ mauhaina phandamānaṃ vinibaddhaṃ mānaina phandamānaṃ parāmaṭṭhaṃ diṭṭhiyā phandamānaṃ vikkhaipagataṃ uddhac‍caina phandamānaṃ aniṭṭhaṅgataṃ vicikicchāya phandamānaṃ thāmagataṃ anusayaihi phandamānaṃ lābhaina phandamānaṃ alābhaina phandamānaṃ yasaina phandamānaṃ ayasaina phandamānaṃ pasaṃsāya phandamānaṃ nindāya phandamānaṃ sukhaina phandamānaṃ dukkhaina phandamānaṃ jātiyā phandamānaṃ jarāya phandamānaṃ byādhinā phandamānaṃ maraṇaina phandamānaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi phandamānaṃ nairayikaina dukkhaina phandamānaṃ tiracchānayaunikaina dukkhaina phandamānaṃ paittivisayikaina dukkhaina phandamānaṃ mānusikaina dukkhaina phandamānaṃ gabbhauk‍kantimūlakaina dukkhaina… gabbhaṭṭhitimūlakaina gabbhaiṭhitīmūlakaina (ka॰) mahāni॰ 11 dukkhaina… gabbhāvuṭṭhānamūlakaina dukkhaina… jātassūpanibandhakaina dukkhaina… jātassa parādhaiyyakaina dukkhaina… attūpak‍kamaina dukkhaina… parūpak‍kamaina dukkhaina… dukkhadukkhaina… saṅkhāradukkhaina… vipariṇāmadukkhaina… cakkhuraugaina dukkhaina… sautaraugaina … ghānaraugaina… jivhāraugaina … kāyaraugaina… sīsaraugaina … kaṇṇaraugaina… mukharaugaina… dantaraugaina… kāsaina… sāsaina… pināsaina… ḍāhaina… jaraina… kucchiraugaina… mucchāya… pakkhandikāya… sūlāya… visūcikāya… kuṭṭhaina… gaṇḍaina… kilāsaina… sausaina… apamāraina… dadduyā… kaṇḍuyā… kacchuyā… rakhasāya… vitacchikāya… lauhitaina… pittaina… madhumaihaina… aṃsāya… pīḷakāya… bhagandalaina… pittasamuṭṭhānaina ābādhaina… saimhasamuṭṭhānaina ābādhaina… vātasamuṭṭhānaina ābādhaina… san‍nipātikaina ābādhaina… utupariṇāmajaina ābādhaina… visamaparihārajaina ābādhaina … aupak‍kamikaina ābādhaina… kammavipākajaina ābādhaina… sītaina… uṇhaina… jighacchāya… pipāsāya… uc‍cāraina… passāvaina… ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassaina dukkhaina… mātumaraṇaina dukkhaina… pitumaraṇaina dukkhaina… bhātumaraṇaina dukkhaina… bhaginimaraṇaina dukkhaina… puttamaraṇaina dukkhaina… dhītumaraṇaina dukkhaina… ñātimaraṇaina dukkhaina… bhaugabyasanaina dukkhaina… raugabyasanaina dukkhaina… sīlabyasanaina dukkhaina… diṭṭhibyasanaina dukkhaina phandamānaṃ samphandamānaṃ vipphandamānaṃ vaidhamānaṃ pavaidhamānaṃ sampavaidhamānaṃ| disvāti disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti – phandamānaṃ pajaṃ disvā|

macchai appaudakai yathāti yathā macchā appaudakai udakapariyādānai kākaihi vā kulalaihi vā balākāhi vā  paripātiyamānā ukkhipiyamānā khaj‍jamānā phandanti samphandanti vipphandanti vaidhanti pavaidhanti sampavaidhanti; aivamaiva pajā taṇhāphandanāya phandanti…pai॰… diṭṭhibyasanaina dukkhaina phandanti samphandanti vipphandanti vaidhanti pavaidhanti sampavaidhantīti – macchai appaudakai yathā|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi byāruddhaiti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sattā viruddhā paṭiviruddhā āhatā pac‍cāhatā āghātitā pac‍cāghātitā| rājānaupi rājūhi vivadanti, khattiyāpi khattiyaihi vivadanti, brāhmaṇāpi brāhmaṇaihi vivadanti, gahapatīpi gahapatīhi vivadanti, mātāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi mātarā vivadati, pitāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi pitarā vivadati, bhātāpi bhātarā vivadati, bhaginīpi bhaginiyā vivadati, bhātāpi bhaginiyā vivadati, bhaginipi bhātarā vivadati, sahāyaupi sahāyaina vivadati; tai tattha kalahaviggahavivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pāṇīhipi upak‍kamanti, laiḍḍūhipi upak‍kamanti, daṇḍaihipi upak‍kamanti, satthaihipi upak‍kamanti, tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti maraṇamattampi dukkhanti – añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi byāruddhai|

disvā  maṃ bhayamāvisīti| disvāti disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā bhayaṃ pīḷanaṃ ghaṭṭanaṃ upaddavau upasaggau āvisīti āvīsīti (sī॰), āvisatīti (syā॰) – disvā maṃ bhayamāvisi|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘phandamānaṃ  pajaṃ disvā, macchai appaudakai yathā|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi byāruddhai, disvā maṃ bhayamāvisī’’ti||

172.

 samantamasārau laukau, disā sabbā samairitā|

icchaṃ bhavanamattanau, nāddasāsiṃ anausitaṃ||

samantamasārau laukauti| laukauti nirayalaukau tiracchānayaunilaukau paittivisayalaukau manussalaukau daivalaukau, khandhalaukau dhātulaukau āyatanalaukau, ayaṃ laukau parau laukau, brahmalaukau daivalaukau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati laukau| nirayalaukau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau nic‍casārasāraina vā sukhasārasāraina vā attasārasāraina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā| tiracchānayaunilaukau…pai॰… paittivisayalaukau… manussalaukau… daivalaukau… khandhalaukau… dhātulaukau… āyatanalaukau… ayaṃ laukau… parau laukau… brahmalaukau… daivalaukau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau nic‍casārasāraina vā sukhasārasāraina vā attasārasāraina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā|

yathā pana naḷau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau, yathā airaṇḍau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau, yathā udumbarau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau, yathā saitakacchau  asārau nissārau sārāpagatau, yathā pāribhaddakau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau, yathā phaiṇapiṇḍau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau, yathā udakapubbuḷaṃ bubbulakaṃ (sī॰), pubbuḷakaṃ (syā॰) asāraṃ nissāraṃ sārāpagataṃ, yathā marīci asārā nissārā sārāpagatā, yathā kadalikkhandhau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau, yathā māyā asārā nissārā sārāpagatā; aivamaiva nirayalaukau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau nic‍casārasāraina  vā sukhasārasāraina vā attasārasāraina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā|

tiracchānayaunilaukau… paittivisayalaukau… manussalaukau… daivalaukau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau  nic‍casārasāraina vā sukhasārasāraina vā attasārasāraina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vā| khandhalaukau… dhātulaukau… āyatanalaukau… ayaṃ laukau… parau laukau… brahmalaukau… daivalaukau asārau nissārau sārāpagatau nic‍casārasāraina vā sukhasārasāraina vā attasārasāraina vā nic‍caina vā dhuvaina vā sassataina vā avipariṇāmadhammaina vāti – samantamasārau laukau|

disā sabbā samairitāti| yai puratthimāya disāya saṅkhārā, taipi airitā samairitā calitā ghaṭṭitā anic‍catāya jātiyā anugatā jarāya anusaṭā byādhinā abhibhūtā maraṇaina abbhāhatā dukkhai patiṭṭhitā atāṇā alaiṇā asaraṇā asaraṇībhūtā| yai pacchimāya disāya saṅkhārā…pai॰… yai uttarāya disāya saṅkhārā… yai dakkhiṇāya disāya saṅkhārā… yai puratthimāya anudisāya saṅkhārā… yai pacchimāya anudisāya saṅkhārā… yai uttarāya anudisāya saṅkhārā… yai dakkhiṇāya anudisāya saṅkhārā… yai haiṭṭhimāya disāya saṅkhārā… yai uparimāya disāya saṅkhārā… yai dasasu disāsu saṅkhārā, taipi airitā samairitā calitā ghaṭṭitā anic‍catāya jātiyā anugatā jarāya anusaṭā byādhinā abhibhūtā maraṇaina  abbhāhatā dukkhai patiṭṭhitā atāṇā alaiṇā asaraṇā asaraṇībhūtā| bhāsitampi caitaṃ –

‘‘kiñ‍cāpi  caitaṃ jalatī vimānaṃ, aubhāsayaṃ uttariyaṃ disāya|

rūpai raṇaṃ disvā sadā pavaidhitaṃ, tasmā na rūpai ramatī sumaidhau||

‘‘mac‍cunābbhāhatau laukau, jarāya parivāritau|

taṇhāsal‍laina autiṇṇau, icchādhūmāyitau icchādhumāyikau (syā॰) sadā||

‘‘sabbau ādīpitau  laukau, sabbau laukau padhūpitau|

sabbau paj‍jalitau laukau, sabbau laukau pakampitau’’ti||

disā sabbā samairitā|

icchaṃ bhavanamattanauti| attanau bhavanaṃ tāṇaṃ laiṇaṃ saraṇaṃ gatiṃ parāyanaṃ icchantau sādiyantau patthayantau pihayantau abhijappantauti – icchaṃ bhavanamattanau| nāddasāsiṃ anausitanti| ajjhausitaṃyaiva addasaṃ, anajjhausitaṃ nāddasaṃ, sabbaṃ yaubbañ‍ñaṃ jarāya ausitaṃ, sabbaṃ  āraugyaṃ byādhinā ausitaṃ, sabbaṃ jīvitaṃ maraṇaina ausitaṃ, sabbaṃ lābhaṃ alābhaina ausitaṃ, sabbaṃ yasaṃ ayasaina ausitaṃ, sabbaṃ pasaṃsaṃ nindāya ausitaṃ, sabbaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaina ausitaṃ|

‘‘lābhau  alābhau yasau ayasau ca, nindā pasaṃsā ca sukhaṃ dukhañ‍ca|

aitai anic‍cā manujaisu dhammā, asassatā vipariṇāmadhammā’’ti||

nāddasāsiṃ anausitaṃ|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘samantamasārau laukau, disā sabbā samairitā|

icchaṃ bhavanamattanau, nāddasāsiṃ anausita’’nti||

173.

 ausānai  tvaiva byāruddhai, disvā mai aratī ahu|

athaittha sal‍lamaddakkhiṃ, duddasaṃ hadayassitaṃ||

ausānai tvaiva byāruddhaiti| ausānai tvaivāti sabbaṃ yaubbañ‍ñaṃ jarā ausāpaiti, sabbaṃ āraugyaṃ byādhi ausāpaiti, sabbaṃ jīvitaṃ maraṇaṃ ausāpaiti, sabbaṃ lābhaṃ alābhau ausāpaiti, sabbaṃ yasaṃ ayasau ausāpaiti, sabbaṃ pasaṃsaṃ nindā ausāpaiti, sabbaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ ausāpaitīti – ausānai tvaiva| byāruddhaiti yaubbañ‍ñakāmā sattā jarāya paṭiviruddhā, āraugyakāmā sattā byādhinā paṭiviruddhā, jīvitukāmā sattā maraṇaina paṭiviruddhā, lābhakāmā sattā alābhaina paṭiviruddhā, yasakāmā sattā ayasaina paṭiviruddhā, pasaṃsakāmā sattā nindāya paṭiviruddhā, sukhakāmā sattā dukkhaina paṭiviruddhā āhatā pac‍cāhatā āghātitā pac‍cāghātitāti – ausānai tvaiva byāruddhai|

disvā  mai aratī ahūti| disvāti disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti – disvā| mai aratīti yā arati yā anabhirati yā anabhiramanā yā uk‍kaṇṭhitā yā paritasitā ahūti – disvā mai aratī ahu|

athaittha sal‍lamaddakkhinti| athāti padasandhi…pai॰… padānupubbatāpaitaṃ – athāti| aitthāti  sattaisu| sal‍lanti satta sal‍lāni – rāgasal‍laṃ, dausasal‍laṃ, mauhasal‍laṃ, mānasal‍laṃ, diṭṭhisal‍laṃ, saukasal‍laṃ, kathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ| addakkhinti addasaṃ adakkhiṃ apassiṃ paṭivijjhinti – athaittha sal‍lamaddakkhiṃ|

duddasaṃ hadayassitanti| duddasanti duddasaṃ duddakkhaṃ duppassaṃ dubbujjhaṃ duranubujjhaṃ duppaṭivijjhanti – duddasaṃ| hadayassitanti hadayaṃ vuc‍cati cittaṃ| yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| hadayassitanti hadayanissitaṃ cittasitaṃ cittanissitaṃ cittaina sahagataṃ sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ aikuppādaṃ aikaniraudhaṃ aikavatthukaṃ  aikārammaṇanti – duddasaṃ hadayassitaṃ|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘ausānai tvaiva byāruddhai, disvā mai aratī ahu|

athaittha sal‍lamaddakkhiṃ, duddasaṃ hadayassita’’nti||

174.

 yaina sal‍laina autiṇṇau, disā sabbā vidhāvati|

tamaiva sal‍lamabbuyha, na dhāvati na sīdati||

yaina  sal‍laina autiṇṇau, disā sabbā vidhāvatīti| sal‍lanti| satta sal‍lāni – rāgasal‍laṃ, dausasal‍laṃ, mauhasal‍laṃ, mānasal‍laṃ, diṭṭhisal‍laṃ, saukasal‍laṃ, kathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ| katamaṃ rāgasal‍laṃ? yau rāgau sārāgau anunayau anuraudhau nandirāgau cittassa sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ – idaṃ rāgasal‍laṃ|

katamaṃ dausasal‍laṃ? ‘‘anatthaṃ mai acarī’’ti āghātau jāyati ‘‘anatthaṃ mai caratī’’ti āghātau jāyati, ‘‘anatthaṃ mai carissatī’’ti āghātau jāyati…pai॰… caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau anattamanatā cittassa – idaṃ dausasal‍laṃ|

katamaṃ mauhasal‍laṃ? dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇaṃ, pubbantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, aparantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, pubbantāparantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu añ‍ñāṇaṃ| yaṃ aivarūpaṃ adassanaṃ anabhisamayau ananubaudhau asambaudhau appaṭivaidhau asaṅgāhaṇā apariyaugāhaṇā asaṅgāhatā apariyaugāhatā (syā॰), asaṅgāhanā apariyaugāhanā (ka॰) asamapaikkhanā apac‍cavaikkhanā apac‍cakkhakammaṃ  dummaijjhaṃ bālyaṃ mauhau pamauhau sammauhau avij‍jā avij‍jaughau avij‍jāyaugau avij‍jānusayau avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – idaṃ mauhasal‍laṃ|

katamaṃ mānasal‍laṃ? ‘‘saiyyauhamasmī’’ti mānau, ‘‘sadisauhamasmī’’ti mānau, ‘‘hīnauhamasmī’’ti mānau| yau aivarūpau mānau mañ‍ñanā mañ‍ñitattaṃ  un‍nati un‍namau dhajau sampaggāhau kaitukamyatā cittassa – idaṃ mānasal‍laṃ|

katamaṃ diṭṭhisal‍laṃ? vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, dasavatthukā  micchādiṭṭhi, dasavatthukā antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| yā aivarūpā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantārau diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisañ‍ñaujanaṃ gāhau paṭiggāhau abhinivaisau parāmāsau kummaggau kumaggau (ka॰) mahāni॰ 38 micchāpathau micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau viparītaggāhau vipal‍lāsaggāhau micchāgāhau ayāthāvakasmiṃ ‘‘yāthāvaka’’nti gāhau yāvatā dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni – idaṃ diṭṭhisal‍laṃ|

katamaṃ saukasal‍laṃ? ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa sīlabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa diṭṭhibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa saukau saucanā saucitattaṃ antausaukau antauparisaukau antauḍāhau antaupariḍāhau caitasau parijjhāyanā daumanassaṃ – idaṃ saukasal‍laṃ|

katamaṃ kathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ? dukkhai kaṅkhā, dukkhasamudayai kaṅkhā, dukkhaniraudhai kaṅkhā, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya kaṅkhā, pubbantai kaṅkhā, aparantai kaṅkhā, pubbantāparantai kaṅkhā, idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu kaṅkhā| yā aivarūpā kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā kaṅkhāyitattaṃ vimati vicikicchā dvaiḷhakaṃ dvaidhāpathau saṃsayau anaikaṃsaggāhau  āsappanā parisappanā apariyaugāhaṇā chambhitattaṃ cittassa manauvilaikhau – idaṃ kathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ|

yaina  sal‍laina autiṇṇau, disā sabbā vidhāvatīti| rāgasal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati, pāṇampi hanati, adin‍nampi ādiyati, sandhimpi chindati, nil‍laupampi harati, aikāgārikampi karauti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati, paradārampi gacchati, musāpi bhaṇati; aivampi rāgasal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau dhāvati vidhāvati  sandhāvati saṃsarati| atha vā rāgasal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau bhaugai pariyaisantau nāvāya mahāsamuddaṃ pakkhandati| sītassa  purakkhatau uṇhassa purakkhatau ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassaihi pīḷiyamānau rissamānau (sī॰ syā॰) khuppipāsāya miyyamānau tigumbaṃ gacchati, tak‍kaulaṃ gacchati, tak‍kasīlaṃ gacchati, kāḷamukhaṃ gacchati, purapūraṃ gacchati, vaisuṅgaṃ gacchati, vairāpathaṃ gacchati, javaṃ gacchati, tāmaliṃ gacchati, vaṅkaṃ gacchati, aiḷabandhanaṃ gacchati, suvaṇṇakūṭaṃ gacchati, suvaṇṇabhūmiṃ gacchati, tambapaṇṇiṃ gacchati, suppādakaṃ suppārakaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), suppāraṃ (syā॰) gacchati, bhārukacchaṃ gacchati, suraṭṭhaṃ gacchati, bhaṅgalaukaṃ aṅgalaukaṃ (sī॰), aṅgaṇaikaṃ (syā॰) gacchati, bhaṅgaṇaṃ gaṅgaṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) gacchati, paramabhaṅgaṇaṃ gacchati, yaunaṃ gacchati, paramayaunaṃ gacchati, vinakaṃ gacchati, mūlapadaṃ gacchati, marukantāraṃ gacchati, jaṇṇupathaṃ gacchati, ajapathaṃ gacchati, maiṇḍapathaṃ gacchati, saṅkupathaṃ gacchati, chattapathaṃ gacchati, vaṃsapathaṃ gacchati, sakuṇapathaṃ gacchati, mūsikapathaṃ gacchati, daripathaṃ gacchati, vaittācāraṃ gacchati; pariyaisantau na labhati, alābhamūlakampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, pariyaisantau labhati, laddhā ārakkhamūlakampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti ‘‘kinti mai bhaugai naiva rājānau haraiyyuṃ na caurā haraiyyuṃ na aggi dahaiyya na udakaṃ vahaiyya na appiyā dāyādā haraiyyu’’nti| tassa aivaṃ ārakkhatau gaupayatau tai bhaugā vippaluj‍janti, sau vippayaugamūlakampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivampi rāgasal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau dhāvati vidhāvati sandhāvati saṃsarati|

dausasal‍laina…pai॰… mauhasal‍laina… mānasal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya  duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati, pāṇampi hanati, adin‍nampi ādiyati, sandhimpi chindati, nil‍laupampi harati, aikāgārikampi karauti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati, paradārampi gacchati, musāpi bhaṇati| aivaṃ mānasal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau dhāvati vidhāvati sandhāvati saṃsarati|

diṭṭhisal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau acailakau hauti muttācārau hatthāpalaikhanau hatthāvalaikhanau (syā॰), na aihibhadantikau, na tiṭṭhabhadantikau; nābhihaṭaṃ, na uddissakataṃ, na nimantanaṃ sādiyati, sau na kumbhimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na kaḷaupimukhā khaḷaupimukhā (sī॰) paṭiggaṇhāti, na aiḷakamantaraṃ, na daṇḍamantaraṃ, na musalamantaraṃ, na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ, na gabbhiniyā, na pāyamānāya, na purisantaragatāya, na saṃkittīsu, na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti, na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī| na macchaṃ na maṃsaṃ na suraṃ na mairayaṃ na thusaudakaṃ pivati| sau  aikāgārikau vā hauti aikālaupikau, dvāgārikau vā hauti dvālaupikau…pai॰… sattāgārikau vā hauti sattālaupikau| aikissāpi bhattiyā yāpaiti, dvīhipi bhattīhi yāpaiti…pai॰… sattahipi bhattīhi yāpaiti| aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti, dvīhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti…pai॰… sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti| iti aivarūpaṃ aḍḍhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| aivampi diṭṭhisal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau dhāvati vidhāvati sandhāvati saṃsarati|

atha vā diṭṭhisal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau sau sākabhakkhau vā hauti, sāmākabhakkhau vā hauti, nīvārabhakkhau vā hauti, daddulabhakkhau vā hauti, haṭabhakkhau vā hauti, kaṇabhakkhau vā hauti, ācāmabhakkhau vā  hauti, piñ‍ñākabhakkhau vā hauti, tilabhakkhau vā hauti, tiṇabhakkhau vā hauti, gaumayabhakkhau vā hauti, vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujanau| sau sāṇānipi dhāraiti, masāṇānipi  dhāraiti, chavadussānipi dhāraiti, paṃsukūlānipi dhāraiti, tirīṭānipi dhāraiti, ajinānipi dhāraiti, ajinakkhipampi dhāraiti, kusacīrampi dhāraiti, vākacīrampi  dhāraiti, phalakacīrampi dhāraiti, kaisakambalampi dhāraiti, ulūkapakkhampi dhāraiti, kaisamassulaucakaupi hauti, kaisamassulaucanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| ubbhaṭṭhakaupi hauti āsanapaṭikkhittau, uk‍kuṭikaupi hauti uk‍kuṭikappadhānamanuyuttau, kaṇṭakāpassayikau hauti, kaṇṭakāpassayai saiyyaṃ kappaiti, phalakasaiyyampi kappaiti, thaṇḍilasaiyyampi kappaiti, aikāpassayikau hauti rajaujal‍ladharau, abbhaukāsikaupi hauti yathāsanthatikau, vaikaṭikaupi hauti vikaṭabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau, apānakaupi hauti apānakattamanuyuttau, sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| iti aivarūpaṃ anaikavihitaṃ kāyassa ātāpanaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| aivampi diṭṭhisal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau dhāvati vidhāvati sandhāvati saṃsarati|

saukasal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imissāyaiva sāvatthiyā añ‍ñatarissā itthiyā mātā kālamakāsi| sā tassā kālaṃ kiriyāya ummattikā khittacittā rathiyāya rathiyaṃ, siṅghāṭakaina  siṅghāṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāha – ‘api mai mātaraṃ addassatha, api mai mātaraṃ addassathā’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imissāyaiva sāvatthiyā añ‍ñatarissā  itthiyā pitā kālamakāsi… bhātā kālamakāsi… bhaginī kālamakāsi… puttau kālamakāsi… dhītā kālamakāsi… sāmikau kālamakāsi| sā tassa kālaṃ kiriyāya ummattikā khittacittā rathiyāya rathiyaṃ, siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāha – ‘api mai sāmikaṃ addassatha, api mai sāmikaṃ addassathā’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imissāyaiva sāvatthiyā añ‍ñatarassa purisassa mātā kālamakāsi| sau tassā kālaṃ kiriyāya ummattakau khittacittau rathiyāya rathiyaṃ, siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāha – ‘api mai mātaraṃ addassatha, api mai mātaraṃ addassathā’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imissāyaiva sāvatthiyā añ‍ñatarassa purisassa pitā kālamakāsi… bhātā kālamakāsi… bhaginī kālamakāsi… puttau kālamakāsi… dhītā kālamakāsi… pajāpati kālamakāsi| sau tassā kālaṃ kiriyāya ummattakau khittacittau rathiyāya rathiyaṃ, siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāha – ‘api mai pajāpatiṃ addassatha, api mai pajāpatiṃ addassathā’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imissāyaiva sāvatthiyā añ‍ñatarā itthī ñātikulaṃ agamāsi| tassā tai ñātakā sāmikaṃ acchinditvā añ‍ñassa dātukāmā, sā ca naṃ na icchati| atha khau sā itthī sāmikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘imai, ayyaputta, ñātakā tava acchinditvā añ‍ñassa dātukāmā, ubhau mayaṃ  marissāmā’ti| atha khau sau purisau taṃ itthiṃ dvidhā chaitvā attānaṃ aupātaiti – ‘ubhau paic‍ca bhavissāmā’’’ti| aivaṃ saukasal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau dhāvati vidhāvati sandhāvati saṃsarati|

kathaṃkathāsal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau saṃsayapakkhandau hauti vimatipakkhandau dvaiḷhakajātau – ‘‘ahausiṃ nu khau ahaṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, nanu khau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ nu khau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, kathaṃ nu khau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ hutvā kiṃ ahausiṃ nu khau atītamaddhānaṃ, bhavissāmi nu khau ahaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, nanu  khau bhavissāmi anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ nu khau bhavissāmi anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kathaṃ nu khau bhavissāmi anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ hutvā kiṃ bhavissāmi nu khau anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aitarahi vā pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ ajjhattaṃ kathaṃkathī hauti, ahaṃ nu khausmi, nau nu khausmi, kiṃ nu khausmi kathaṃ nu khausmi, ayaṃ nu khau sattau kutau āgatau, sau kuhiṃ gāmī bhavissatī’’ti| aivaṃ kathaṃkathāsal‍laina autiṇṇau viddhau phuṭṭhau paraitau samauhitau saman‍nāgatau dhāvati vidhāvati sandhāvati saṃsarati|

tai sal‍lai abhisaṅkharauti; tai sal‍lai abhisaṅkharauntau sal‍lābhisaṅkhāravasaina puratthimaṃ disaṃ dhāvati, pacchimaṃ disaṃ dhāvati, uttaraṃ disaṃ dhāvati, dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ dhāvati| tai sal‍lābhisaṅkhārā appahīnā; sal‍lābhisaṅkhārānaṃ appahīnattā gatiyā dhāvati, nirayai dhāvati, tiracchānayauniyā dhāvati, paittivisayai dhāvati, manussalaukai dhāvati, daivalaukai dhāvati, gatiyā  gatiṃ, upapattiyā upapattiṃ, paṭisandhiyā paṭisandhiṃ, bhavaina bhavaṃ, saṃsāraina saṃsāraṃ, vaṭṭaina vaṭṭaṃ dhāvati vidhāvati sandhāvati saṃsaratīti – yaina sal‍laina autiṇṇau disā sabbā vidhāvati|

tamaiva sal‍lamabbuyha, na dhāvati na sīdatīti| tamaiva rāgasal‍laṃ dausasal‍laṃ mauhasal‍laṃ mānasal‍laṃ diṭṭhisal‍laṃ saukasal‍laṃ kathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ abbuyha abbuhitvā uddharitvā samuddharitvā uppāṭayitvā samuppāṭayitvā uppādayitvā samuppādayitvā (syā॰ ka॰) pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantiṃ karitvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvā naiva puratthimaṃ disaṃ dhāvati na pacchimaṃ disaṃ dhāvati na uttaraṃ disaṃ dhāvati na dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ dhāvati| tai sal‍lābhisaṅkhārā pahīnā; sal‍lābhisaṅkhārānaṃ pahīnattā gatiyā na dhāvati, nirayai na dhāvati, tiracchānayauniyā na dhāvati, paittivisayai na dhāvati, manussalaukai na dhāvati, daivalaukai na dhāvati, na gatiyā gatiṃ, na upapattiyā upapattiṃ, na paṭisandhiyā paṭisandhiṃ, na bhavaina bhavaṃ, na saṃsāraina saṃsāraṃ, na vaṭṭaina vaṭṭaṃ dhāvati vidhāvati sandhāvati saṃsaratīti – tamaiva sal‍lamabbuyha na dhāvati| na sīdatīti  kāmaughai na sīdati, bhavaughai na sīdati, diṭṭhaughai na sīdati, avij‍jaughai na sīdati, na saṃsīdati na ausīdati na avasīdati na gacchati na avagacchatīti – tamaiva sal‍lamabbuyha, na dhāvati na sīdati|

taināha  bhagavā –

‘‘yaina sal‍laina autiṇṇau, disā sabbā vidhāvati|

tamaiva sal‍lamabbuyha, na dhāvati na sīdatī’’ti||

175.

 tattha  sikkhānugīyanti, yāni laukai gadhitāni| 

na taisu pasutau siyā, nibbijjha sabbasau kāmai| 

sikkhai nibbānamattanau||

tattha sikkhānugīyanti, yāni laukai gadhitānīti| sikkhāti hatthisikkhā assasikkhā rathasikkhā dhanusikkhā sālākiyaṃ sal‍lakattiyaṃ kāyatikicchaṃ bhūtiyaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ kaumāratikicchaṃ (syā॰), kaumārasac‍caṃ (ka॰)| anugīyantīti gīyanti niggīyanti kathīyanti bhaṇīyanti dīpīyanti vauharīyanti| atha vā gīyanti gaṇhīyanti uggaṇhīyanti  dhārīyanti upadhārīyanti upalakkhīyanti gadhitapaṭilābhāya| gadhitā vuc‍canti pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā – cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasañhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasañhitā rajanīyā| kiṃkāraṇā gadhitā vuc‍canti pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā? yaibhuyyaina daivamanussā pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai icchanti sādiyanti patthayanti pihayanti abhijappanti taṃkāraṇā gadhitā vuc‍canti pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| laukaiti manussalaukaiti – tattha sikkhānugīyanti, yāni laukai gadhitāni|

na taisu pasutau siyāti| tāsu vā sikkhāsu taisu vā pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu na pasutau siyā, na tan‍nin‍nau assa, na tappauṇau, na tappabbhārau, na tadadhimuttau, na tadadhipataiyyauti – na taisu pasutau siyā|

nibbijjha  sabbasau kāmaiti| nibbijjhāti paṭivijjhitvā| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti paṭivijjhitvā, ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti paṭivijjhitvā…pai॰… ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ  taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti paṭivijjhitvā| sabbasauti sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asaisaṃ nissaisaṃ pariyādiyanavacanamaitaṃ – sabbasauti| kāmāti uddānatau dvai kāmā – vatthukāmā ca kilaisakāmā ca…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti vatthukāmā…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti kilaisakāmāti – nibbijjha sabbasau kāmai|

sikkhai nibbānamattanauti| sikkhāti tissau sikkhā – adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā…pai॰… ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| nibbānamattanauti attanau rāgassa nibbāpanāya dausassa nibbāpanāya mauhassa nibbāpanāya…pai॰… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārānaṃ  samāya upasamāya vūpasamāya nibbāpanāya paṭinissaggāya paṭipassaddhiyā adhisīlampi sikkhaiyya adhicittampi sikkhaiyya adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhaiyya, imā tissau sikkhāyau āvaj‍jantau sikkhaiyya jānantau sikkhaiyya…pai॰… sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikarauntau sikkhaiyya ācaraiyya samācaraiyya samādāya vattaiyyāti – sikkhai nibbānamattanau|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘tattha  sikkhānugīyanti, yāni laukai gadhitāni|

na taisu pasutau siyā, nibbijjha sabbasau kāmai|

sikkhai nibbānamattanau’’ti||

176.

 sac‍cau  siyā appagabbhau, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

ak‍kaudhanau laubhapāpaṃ, vaivicchaṃ vitarai muni||

sac‍cau siyā appagabbhauti| sac‍cau siyāti sac‍cavācāya saman‍nāgatau siyā, sammādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau siyā, ariyaina aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina saman‍nāgatau siyāti – sac‍cau siyā| appagabbhauti tīṇi pāgabbhiyāni – kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ, vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ, caitasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ caitasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ| yassimāni tīṇi pāgabbhiyāni pahīnāni samucchin‍nāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni  abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni, sau vuc‍cati appagabbhauti – sac‍cau siyā appagabbhau|

amāyau rittapaisuṇauti| māyā vuc‍cati vañ‍canikā cariyā| idhaikac‍cau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā tassa paṭicchādanahaitu pāpikaṃ icchaṃ paṇidahati, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti icchati, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti saṅkappaiti, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti vācaṃ bhāsati, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti kāyaina parak‍kamati| yā aivarūpā māyā māyāvitā ac‍casarā vañ‍canā nikati nikiraṇā pariharaṇā gūhanā parigūhanā chādanā paricchādanā anuttānīkammaṃ anāvikammaṃ vaucchādanā pāpakiriyā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati māyā| yassaisā māyā pahīnā samucchin‍nā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, sau vuc‍cati  amāyau| rittapaisuṇauti paisuñ‍ñanti idhaikac‍cau pisuṇavācau hauti…pai॰… aivaṃ bhaidādhippāyaina paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati| yassaitaṃ paisuñ‍ñaṃ pahīnaṃ samucchin‍naṃ vūpasantaṃ paṭipassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā  daḍḍhaṃ, sau vuc‍cati rittapaisuṇau vivittapaisuṇau pavivittapaisuṇauti – amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

ak‍kaudhanau laubhapāpaṃ, vaivicchaṃ vitarai munīti| ak‍kaudhanauti hi khau vuttaṃ, api ca kaudhau tāva vattabbau| dasahākāraihi kaudhau  jāyati| ‘‘anatthaṃ mai acarī’’ti kaudhau jāyati…pai॰… yassaisau kaudhau pahīnau samucchin‍nau vūpasantau paṭipassaddhau abhabbuppattikau ñāṇagginā daḍḍhau, sau vuc‍cati ak‍kaudhanau| kaudhassa pahīnattā ak‍kaudhanau, kaudhavatthussa pariñ‍ñātattā ak‍kaudhanau, kaudhahaitussa upacchin‍nattā ak‍kaudhanau| laubhauti yau laubhau lubbhanā lubbhitattaṃ…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| vaivicchaṃ vuc‍cati pañ‍ca macchariyāni – āvāsamacchariyaṃ…pai॰… gāhau vuc‍cati macchariyaṃ| munīti| maunaṃ vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… saṅgajālamatic‍ca sau muni| ak‍kaudhanau laubhapāpaṃ, vaivicchaṃ vitarai munīti| muni laubhapāpañ‍ca vaivicchañ‍ca atari uttari patari samatik‍kami vītivattayīti  vītivattīti (ka॰) – ak‍kaudhanau laubhapāpaṃ, vaivicchaṃ vitarai muni|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘sac‍cau siyā appagabbhau, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

ak‍kaudhanau laubhapāpaṃ, vaivicchaṃ vitarai munī’’ti||

177.

 niddaṃ  tandiṃ sahai thīnaṃ, pamādaina na saṃvasai|

atimānai na tiṭṭhaiyya, nibbānamanasau narau||

niddaṃ tandiṃ sahai thīnanti| niddāti yā kāyassa akalyatā akammañ‍ñatā aunāhau pariyaunāhau antausamauraudhau middhaṃ suppaṃ pacalāyikā suppaṃ suppanā suppitattaṃ| tandinti yā tandī tandi (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) tandiyanā tandiyitattaṃ tandimanakatā ālasyaṃ ālasyāyatā ālasyāyitattaṃ| thīnanti yā cittassa akalyatā akammañ‍ñatā aulīyanā sal‍līyanā līnaṃ līyanā līyitattaṃ, thinaṃ thiyanā thiyitattaṃ cittassa| niddaṃ tandiṃ sahai thīnanti| niddañ‍ca tandiñ‍ca thinañ‍ca sahai sahaiyya parisahaiyya abhibhavaiyya ajjhauttharaiyya pariyādiyaiyya maddaiyyāti – niddaṃ tandiṃ sahai thīnaṃ|

pamādaina na saṃvasaiti| pamādau vattabbau kāyaduc‍caritai vā vacīduc‍caritai vā manauduc‍caritai vā pañ‍casu vā kāmaguṇaisu| cittassa vausaggau vausaggānuppādanaṃ vaussaggau vaussaggānuppādanaṃ (bahūsu) vā kusalānaṃ vā  dhammānaṃ bhāvanāya asak‍kac‍cakiriyatā asātac‍cakiriyatā anaṭṭhitakiriyatā aulīnavuttitā nikkhittacchandatā nikkhittadhuratā anāsaivanā abhāvanā abahulīkammaṃ anadhiṭṭhānaṃ ananuyaugau pamādau| yau aivarūpau pamādau pamaj‍janā pamaj‍jitattaṃ  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati pamādau| pamādaina na saṃvasaiti pamādaina na vasaiyya na saṃvasaiyya na āvasaiyya na parivasaiyya, pamādaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, pamādā āratau assa  viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – pamādaina na saṃvasai|

atimānai  na tiṭṭhaiyyāti| atimānauti idhaikac‍cau paraṃ atimañ‍ñati jātiyā vā gauttaina vā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunā| yau aivarūpau mānau mañ‍ñanā mañ‍ñitattaṃ un‍nati un‍namau dhajau sampaggāhau kaitukamyatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati atimānau| atimānai na tiṭṭhaiyyāti| atimānai na tiṭṭhaiyya na saṃtiṭṭhaiyya, atimānaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, atimānā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – atimānai na tiṭṭhaiyya|

nibbānamanasau narauti| idhaikac‍cau dānaṃ daintau sīlaṃ samādiyantau upausathakammaṃ karauntau pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhapaintau parivaiṇaṃ sammaj‍jantau caitiyaṃ vandantau caitiyai gandhamālaṃ āraupaintau caitiyaṃ padakkhiṇaṃ karauntau yaṃ kiñ‍ci taidhātukaṃ kusalābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauntau na gatihaitu na upapattihaitu na paṭisandhihaitu na bhavahaitu na saṃsārahaitu na vaṭṭahaitu, sabbaṃ taṃ visaṃyaugādhippāyau nibbānanin‍nau nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau abhisaṅkharautīti| aivampi nibbānamanasau narau| atha vā sabbasaṅkhāradhātuyā cittaṃ paṭivāpaitvā paṭivāsaitvā (ka॰) amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘‘aitaṃ santaṃ  aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’’nti| aivampi nibbānamanasau narau|

‘‘na paṇḍitā upadhisukhassa haitu, dadanti dānāni punabbhavāya|

kāmañ‍ca tai upadhiparikkhayāya, dadanti dānaṃ apunabbhavāya||

‘‘na  paṇḍitā upadhisukhassa haitu, bhāvainti jhānāni punabbhavāya|

kāmañ‍ca tai upadhiparikkhayāya, bhāvainti jhānaṃ apunabbhavāya||

‘‘tai  nibbuttiṃ āsisamānasā āsiṃsamānā (sī॰), abhimānā (syā॰) dadanti, tan‍nin‍nacittā tan‍nin‍nā tañ‍cittā (ka॰) tadadhimuttā|

naj‍jau yathā sāgaramajjhupaitā sāgaramajjhagatā (syā॰), bhavanti nibbānaparāyanā tai’’ti||

nibbānamanasau narau| taināha bhagavā –

‘‘niddaṃ tandi sahai thīnaṃ, pamādaina na saṃvasai|

atimānai na tiṭṭhaiyya, nibbānamanasau narau’’ti||

178.

 mausavaj‍jai na niyyaitha, nīyaitha (ka॰) rūpai snaihaṃ na kubbayai|

mānañ‍ca parijānaiyya, sāhasā viratau carai||

mausavaj‍jai  na niyyaithāti| mausavaj‍jaṃ vuc‍cati musāvādau| idhaikac‍cau sabhaggatau vā parisaggatau vā ñātimajjhagatau vā pūgamajjhagatau vā rājakulamajjhagatau vā abhinītau sakkhipuṭṭhau – ‘‘aihambhau purisa, yaṃ jānāsi taṃ vadaihī’’ti, sau ajānaṃ vā āha – ‘‘jānāmī’’ti, jānaṃ vā āha – ‘‘na jānāmī’’ti, apassaṃ vā āha – ‘‘passāmī’’ti, passaṃ vā āha – ‘‘na passāmī’’ti| iti attahaitu vā parahaitu vā āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitu vā sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti – idaṃ vuc‍cati mausavaj‍jaṃ| api ca tīhākāraihi…pai॰… catūhākāraihi… pañ‍cahākāraihi… chahākāraihi… sattahākāraihi… aṭṭhahākāraihi…pai॰… imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi musāvādau hauti| mausavaj‍jai na niyyaithāti| mausavaj‍jai na yāyaiyya na niyyāyaiyya na vahaiyya na saṃharaiyya, mausavaj‍jaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, mausavaj‍jā āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – mausavaj‍jai na niyyaitha|

rūpai  snaihaṃ na kubbayaiti| rūpanti cattārau ca mahābhūtā, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya rūpaṃ| rūpai snaihaṃ na kubbayaiti| rūpai snaihaṃ na karaiyya chandaṃ na karaiyya paimaṃ na karaiyya rāgaṃ na karaiyya na janaiyya na sañ‍janaiyya na nibbattaiyya nābhinibbattaiyyāti – rūpai snaihaṃ na kubbayai|

mānañ‍ca  parijānaiyyāti| mānauti aikavidhaina mānau – yā cittassa  un‍nati| duvidhaina mānau – attuk‍kaṃsanamānau, paravambhanamānau| tividhaina mānau – ‘‘saiyyauhamasmī’’ti mānau, ‘‘sadisauhamasmī’’ti mānau, ‘‘hīnauhamasmī’’ti mānau| catuvidhaina mānau – lābhaina mānaṃ janaiti, yasaina mānaṃ janaiti, pasaṃsāya mānaṃ janaiti, sukhaina mānaṃ janaiti| pañ‍cavidhaina mānau – ‘‘lābhimhi manāpikānaṃ rūpāna’’nti mānaṃ janaiti, ‘‘lābhimhi manāpikānaṃ saddānaṃ…pai॰… gandhānaṃ… rasānaṃ… phauṭṭhabbāna’’nti mānaṃ janaiti| chabbidhaina mānau – cakkhusampadāya mānaṃ janaiti, sautasampadāya…pai॰… ghānasampadāya… jivhāsampadāya… kāyasampadāya… manausampadāya mānaṃ janaiti| sattavidhaina mānau – atimānau, mānātimānau, aumānau, sadisamānau, adhimānau, asmimānau, micchāmānau| aṭṭhavidhaina mānau – lābhaina mānaṃ janaiti, alābhaina aumānaṃ janaiti, yasaina mānaṃ janaiti, ayasaina aumānaṃ janaiti, pasaṃsāya mānaṃ janaiti, nindāya aumānaṃ janaiti, sukhaina mānaṃ janaiti, dukkhaina aumānaṃ janaiti| navavidhaina mānau – saiyyassa ‘‘saiyyauhamasmī’’ti mānau, saiyyassa ‘‘sadisauhamasmī’’ti mānau, saiyyassa ‘‘hīnauhamasmī’’ti mānau, sadisassa ‘‘saiyyauhamasmī’’ti mānau, sadisassa ‘‘sadisauhamasmī’’ti mānau, sadisassa ‘‘hīnauhamasmī’’ti mānau, hīnassa ‘‘saiyyauhamasmī’’ti mānau, hīnassa ‘‘sadisauhamasmī’’ti mānau, hīnassa ‘‘hīnauhamasmī’’ti mānau| dasavidhaina mānau – idhaikac‍cau mānaṃ janaiti jātiyā vā gauttaina vā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunā| yau  aivarūpau mānau mañ‍ñanā mañ‍ñitattaṃ un‍nati un‍namau dhajau sampaggāhau kaitukamyatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati mānau|

mānañ‍ca parijānaiyyāti| mānaṃ tīhi pariñ‍ñāhi parijānaiyya – ñātapariñ‍ñāya, tīraṇapariñ‍ñāya, pahānapariñ‍ñāya| katamā  ñātapariñ‍ñā? mānaṃ jānāti ayaṃ aikavidhaina mānau – yā cittassa un‍nati| ayaṃ duvidhaina mānau – attuk‍kaṃsanamānau paravambhanamānau…pai॰… ayaṃ dasavidhaina mānau  – idhaikac‍cau mānaṃ janaiti jātiyā vā gauttaina vā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā vatthunāti jānāti passati – ayaṃ ñātapariñ‍ñā|

katamā tīraṇapariñ‍ñā? aitaṃ ñātaṃ katvā mānaṃ tīraiti anic‍catau dukkhatau…pai॰… nissaraṇatau tīraiti – ayaṃ tīraṇapariñ‍ñā|

katamā pahānapariñ‍ñā? aivaṃ tīrayitvā mānaṃ pajahati vinaudaiti byantiṃ karauti anabhāvaṃ  gamaiti – ayaṃ pahānapariñ‍ñā| mānañ‍ca parijānaiyyāti mānaṃ imāhi tīhi pariñ‍ñāhi parijānaiyyāti – mānañ‍ca parijānaiyya|

sāhasā viratau caraiti| katamā sāhasā cariyā? rattassa rāgacariyā sāhasā cariyā, duṭṭhassa dausacariyā sāhasā cariyā, mūḷhassa mauhacariyā sāhasā cariyā, vinibaddhassa mānacariyā sāhasā cariyā, parāmaṭṭhassa diṭṭhicariyā sāhasā cariyā, vikkhaipagatassa uddhac‍cacariyā sāhasā cariyā, aniṭṭhaṅgatassa vicikicchācariyā sāhasā cariyā, thāmagatassa anusayacariyā sāhasā cariyā – ayaṃ sāhasā cariyā| sāhasā viratau  caraiti sāhasā cariyāya āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyya caraiyya vicaraiyya iriyaiyya vattaiyya pālaiyya yapaiyya yāpaiyyāti – sāhasā viratau carai|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘mausavaj‍jai na niyyaitha, rūpai snaihaṃ na kubbayai|

mānañ‍ca parijānaiyya, sāhasā viratau carai’’ti||

179.

 purāṇaṃ nābhinandaiyya, navai khantiṃ na kubbayai khantimakubbayai (bahūsu) |

hīyamānai na saucaiyya, ākāsaṃ ākassaṃ (syā॰) na sitau siyā||

purāṇaṃ  nābhinandaiyyāti| purāṇaṃ vuc‍cati atītā rūpavaidanāsañ‍ñāsaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇā| atītai saṅkhārai taṇhāvasaina diṭṭhivasaina nābhinandaiyya nābhivadaiyya na ajjhausaiyya, abhinandanaṃ abhivadanaṃ ajjhausānaṃ gāhaṃ parāmāsaṃ  abhinivaisaṃ pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyyāti – purāṇaṃ nābhinandaiyya|

navai khantiṃ na kubbayaiti| navā vuc‍cati pac‍cuppan‍nā rūpavaidanāsañ‍ñāsaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇā| pac‍cuppan‍nai saṅkhārai taṇhāvasaina diṭṭhivasaina khantiṃ na karaiyya chandaṃ na karaiyya paimaṃ na karaiyya rāgaṃ na karaiyya na janaiyya na sañ‍janaiyya na nibbattaiyya nābhinibbattaiyyāti – navai khantiṃ na kubbayai|

hīyamānai  na saucaiyyāti| hīyamānai hāyamānai parihāyamānai  vaimānai vigacchamānai antaradhāyamānai na saucaiyya na kilamaiyya na parāmasaiyya na paridaivaiyya na urattāḷiṃ kandaiyya na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| cakkhusmiṃ hīyamānai hāyamānai parihāyamānai vaimānai vigacchamānai antaradhāyamānai, sautasmiṃ…pai॰… ghānasmiṃ… jivhāya… kāyasmiṃ… rūpasmiṃ… saddasmiṃ… gandhasmiṃ… rasasmiṃ… phauṭṭhabbasmiṃ… kulasmiṃ… gaṇasmiṃ… āvāsasmiṃ… lābhasmiṃ… yasasmiṃ… pasaṃsāya… sukhasmiṃ… cīvarasmiṃ… piṇḍapātasmiṃ… saināsanasmiṃ… gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārasmiṃ hīyamānai hāyamānai parihāyamānai vaimānai vigacchamānai antaradhāyamānai na saucaiyya na kilamaiyya na parāmasaiyya na paridaivaiyya na urattāḷiṃ kandaiyya na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti – hīyamānai na saucaiyya|

ākāsaṃ na sitau siyāti| ākāsaṃ vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| kiṃkāraṇā ākāsaṃ vuc‍cati taṇhā? yāya taṇhāya rūpaṃ ākassati samākassati gaṇhāti parāmasati abhinivisati, vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… gatiṃ… upapattiṃ… paṭisandhiṃ… bhavaṃ… saṃsāraṃ… vaṭṭaṃ ākassati samākassati gaṇhāti parāmasati abhinivisati; taṃkāraṇā ākāsaṃ vuc‍cati taṇhā| ākāsaṃ na sitau siyāti| taṇhānissitau na siyā| taṇhaṃ  pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantiṃ karaiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyya, taṇhāya āratau assa viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau  vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharaiyyāti – ākāsaṃ na sitau siyā|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘purāṇaṃ nābhinandaiyya, navai khantiṃ na kubbayai|

hīyamānai na saucaiyya, ākāsaṃ na sitau siyā’’ti||

180.

 gaidhaṃ  brūmi mahaughauti, ājavaṃ ācamaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) brūmi jappanaṃ|

ārammaṇaṃ pakampanaṃ, pakappanaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) kāmapaṅkau durac‍cayau||

gaidhaṃ brūmi mahaughautīti| gaidhau vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| mahaughau vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| gaidhaṃ brūmi mahaughautīti| gaidhaṃ ‘‘mahaughau’’ti brūmi ācikkhāmi daisaimi pañ‍ñapaimi paṭṭhapaimi vivarāmi vibhajāmi uttānīkaraumi pakāsaimīti – gaidhaṃ brūmi mahaughauti|

ājavaṃ  brūmi jappananti| ājavā vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| jappanāpi vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| ājavaṃ brūmi jappananti ājavaṃ ‘‘jappanā’’ti brūmi ācikkhāmi…pai॰… uttānīkaraumi pakāsaimīti – ājavaṃ brūmi jappanaṃ|

ārammaṇaṃ pakampananti| ārammaṇampi vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau  rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| pakampanāpi vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlanti – ārammaṇaṃ pakampanaṃ|

kāmapaṅkau durac‍cayauti| kāmapaṅkau kāmakaddamau kāmakilaisau kāmapalipau kāmapalibaudhau kāmapaliraudhau (sī॰ ka॰) durac‍cayau durativattau duttarau duppatarau dussamatik‍kamau dubbītivattauti – kāmapaṅkau durac‍cayau|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘gaidhaṃ  brūmi mahaughauti, ājavaṃ brūmi jappanaṃ|

ārammaṇaṃ pakampanaṃ, kāmapaṅkau durac‍cayau’’ti||

181.

 sac‍cā avauk‍kamaṃ muni, thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau|

sabbaṃ sau paṭinissaj‍ja, sa vai santauti vuc‍cati||

sac‍cā avauk‍kamaṃ munīti| sac‍cavācāya avauk‍kamantau, sammādiṭṭhiyā avauk‍kamantau, ariyā aṭṭhaṅgikā maggā avauk‍kamantau| munīti| maunaṃ vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… saṅgajālamatic‍ca sau munīti – sac‍cā avauk‍kamaṃ muni|

thalai  tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇauti| thalaṃ vuc‍cati amataṃ nibbānaṃ| yau sau sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| brāhmaṇauti sattan‍naṃ dhammānaṃ bāhitattā brāhmaṇau…pai॰… asitau tādi pavuc‍catai sa brahmā| thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇauti| thalai tiṭṭhati dīpai tiṭṭhati tāṇai  tiṭṭhati laiṇai tiṭṭhati saraṇai tiṭṭhati abhayai tiṭṭhati ac‍cutai tiṭṭhati amatai tiṭṭhati nibbānai tiṭṭhatīti – thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau|

sabbaṃ sau paṭinissaj‍jāti| sabbaṃ vuc‍cati dvādasāyatanāni – cakkhu caiva rūpā ca  …pai॰… manau caiva dhammā ca| yatau ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu chandarāgau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau, aittāvatāpi sabbaṃ cattaṃ hauti vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ| yatau taṇhā ca diṭṭhi ca mānau ca pahīnā haunti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, aittāvatāpi sabbaṃ cattaṃ hauti vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ| yatau puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau ca apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau ca ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau ca pahīnā haunti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, aittāvatāpi sabbaṃ cattaṃ hauti vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhanti – sabbaṃ sau paṭinissaj‍ja|

sa vai santauti vuc‍catīti| sau santau upasantau vūpasantau nibbutau paṭipassaddhauti vuc‍cati kathīyati bhaṇīyati dīpīyati vauharīyatīti – sa vai santauti vuc‍cati|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘sac‍cā avauk‍kamaṃ muni, thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau|

sabbaṃ sau paṭinissaj‍ja, sa vai santauti vuc‍catī’’ti||

182.

 sa  vai vidvā viddhā (syā॰) sa vaidagū, ñatvā dhammaṃ anissitau|

sammā sau laukai iriyānau, na pihaitīdha kassaci||

sa vai vidvā sa vaidagūti| vidvāti vidvā vij‍jāgatau ñāṇī vibhāvī maidhāvī| vaidagūti| vaidā vuc‍canti catūsu maggaisu ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… sabbavaidanāsu vītarāgau sabbavaidamatic‍ca vaidagū sauti – sa vai vidvā sa vaidagū|

ñatvā  dhammaṃ anissitauti| ñatvāti ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā, ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā| anissitauti dvai nissayā – taṇhānissayau ca diṭṭhinissayau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhānissayau…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhinissayau| taṇhānissayaṃ pahāya diṭṭhinissayaṃ paṭinissaj‍jitvā cakkhuṃ anissitau… sautaṃ anissitau… ghānaṃ anissitau…pai॰… diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbai  dhammai anissitau anal‍līnau anupagatau anajjhausitau anadhimuttau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharatīti – ñatvā dhammaṃ anissitau|

sammā sau laukai iriyānauti| yatau ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu chandarāgau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau  āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau, aittāvatāpi sammā  sau laukai carati viharati iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti…pai॰… yatau puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau ca apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau ca ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau ca pahīnā haunti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, aittāvatāpi sammā sau laukai carati viharati iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – sammā sau laukai iriyānau|

na pihaitīdha kassacīti| pihā vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| yassaisā pihā taṇhā pahīnā samucchin‍nā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, sau kassaci na pihaiti khattiyassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā vaissassa vā suddassa vā gahaṭṭhassa vā pabbajitassa vā daivassa vā manussassa vāti – na pihaitīdha kassaci|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘sa vai vidvā sa vaidagū, ñatvā dhammaṃ anissitau|

sammā sau laukai iriyānau, na pihaitīdha kassacī’’ti||

183.

 yaudha kāmai ac‍catari, saṅgaṃ laukai durac‍cayaṃ|

na sau saucati nājjhaiti, chin‍nasautau abandhanau||

yaudha  kāmai ac‍catari, saṅgaṃ laukai durac‍cayanti| yauti yau yādisau yathāyuttau yathāvihitau yathāpakārau yaṃ ṭhānappattau yaṃ  dhammasaman‍nāgatau khattiyau vā brāhmaṇau vā vaissau vā suddau vā gahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā daivau vā manussau vā| kāmāti uddānatau dvai kāmā – vatthukāmā ca kilaisakāmā ca…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti vatthukāmā…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti kilaisakāmā| saṅgāti satta saṅgā – rāgasaṅgau, dausasaṅgau, mauhasaṅgau, mānasaṅgau, diṭṭhisaṅgau, kilaisasaṅgau, duc‍caritasaṅgau| laukaiti apāyalaukai manussalaukai daivalaukai khandhalaukai dhātulaukai  āyatanalaukai| saṅgaṃ laukai durac‍cayanti| yau kāmai ca saṅgai ca laukai durac‍cayai durativattai duttarai duppatarai dussamatik‍kamai dubbītivattai atari uttari patari samatik‍kami vītivattayīti – yaudha kāmai ac‍catari, saṅgaṃ laukai durac‍cayaṃ|

na  sau saucati nājjhaitīti| vipariṇataṃ vā vatthuṃ na saucati, vipariṇatasmiṃ vā vatthusmiṃ na saucati| ‘‘cakkhu mai vipariṇata’’nti na saucati…pai॰… sautaṃ mai… ghānaṃ mai… jivhā mai… kāyau mai… rūpā mai… saddā mai… gandhā mai… rasā mai… phauṭṭhabbā mai… kulaṃ mai… gaṇau mai… āvāsau mai… lābhau mai… yasau mai… pasaṃsā mai… sukhaṃ mai… cīvaraṃ mai… piṇḍapātau mai… saināsanaṃ mai… gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā mai… mātā mai… pitā mai… bhātā mai… bhaginī mai… puttau mai… dhītā mai… mittā mai… amac‍cā mai… ñātī mai… ‘‘sālauhitā mai vipariṇatā’’ti na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati na urattāḷiṃ kandati na  sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jatīti – na saucati| nājjhaitīti najjhaiti na ajjhaiti na upanijjhāyati na nijjhāyati na pajjhāyati| atha vā na jāyati na jiyyati na miyyati na cavati na upapaj‍jatīti – nājjhaitīti – na sau saucati nājjhaiti|

chin‍nasautau abandhanauti| sautaṃ vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| yassaisā sautā taṇhā pahīnā samucchin‍nā…pai॰… ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, sau vuc‍cati chin‍nasautau| abandhanauti rāgabandhanaṃ dausabandhanaṃ mauhabandhanaṃ mānabandhanaṃ diṭṭhibandhanaṃ kilaisabandhanaṃ duc‍caritabandhanaṃ, yassaitai bandhanā pahīnā samucchin‍nā…pai॰… ñāṇagginā  daḍḍhā, sau vuc‍cati abandhanauti – chin‍nasautau abandhanau|

taināha bhagavā –

‘‘yaudha kāmai ac‍catari, saṅgaṃ laukai durac‍cayaṃ|

na sau saucati nājjhaiti, chin‍nasautau abandhanau’’ti||

184.

 yaṃ pubbai taṃ visausaihi, pacchā tai māhu kiñ‍canaṃ|
